# Current State — single source of truth

**Last verified:** 2026-10-14
**Verified by:** direct count against `src/effects/registry.ts`, `src/instruments/registry.ts`, `src/presets/factory.ts`, `src/sample-library/`, `docs/adr/`, `tests/` and the `public/models/*.manifest.json` reports.

This document is the **single source of truth** for the headline numbers about KYX / Pulse Forge. Older documents in this repo (`RELEASE_ROADMAP.md`, `DSP-ROADMAP.md`, `EDIT-ROADMAP.md`, `INSTRUMENT-ROADMAP.md`, `SCENE-MODE-ROADMAP.md`, `INTENT_ENGINE.md`, `KYX_CURRENT_STATE.md`, `MAINTENANCE_AUDIT_PROGRESS.md`, `PERFORMANCE.md`) may carry their own point-in-time numbers; when those disagree with the figures below, **this document wins** for the question "how many / what ships today?".

For an architecture overview, see `ARCHITECTURE.md` and `docs/adr/`. For a user-facing description of every feature, see `README.md`.

---

| └─ primary Add Effect choices                         |       15 | `CORE_EFFECT_ORDER` — the fifteen daily devices come FIRST in the `✚ ADD FX` grid, before the flagship suites and the long tail. **The claim is ORDER, not grouping** — measured in Chromium 2026-10-15: this surface is `FxAddPopover`, a FLAT grid of all 57 devices with a category filter, not the old `<select>` of 11 optgroups. Its tiles are per-CATEGORY and do NOT track core membership (TONE 6, DYNAMICS 9, CHARACTER 17, MOVEMENT 17, SPACE 8), and the grid scrolls 397px showing 21 of 57 before scrolling — so "three per area" and "fifteen on screen" are both FALSE here and must not be re-asserted from the retired select. All fifteen ARE among the 21 visible without scrolling. The fifteen are: TONE `eq`/`msEq`/`multiband`; DYNAMICS `compressor`/`limiter`/`gate`; CHARACTER `saturation`/`tapeSat`/`drumBuss`; MOVEMENT `haasWidener`/`chorus`/`tremolo`; SPACE `delay`/`reverb`/`duckDelay`. Equal-height grouping in the registry keeps the order readable (3/3/3/3/3). Mastering suites (ZENIT, APEX, SPAN, TIDE, PRUD) and specialty motion (stutter, comb, vowel) and spectral tools (PORTAL, granularFreeze) keep their "MORE ..." registry groups one layer down. Nothing is removed: `ADDITIONAL_EFFECT_GROUPS` derives from `!DEVICE_MENU_EFFECTS`, so all **57** stay discoverable exactly once, and `CORE` + `FLAGSHIP` + `MORE` is what FxAddPopover and the mixer batch bar enumerate. History: 33 → 37 (the four fundamentals were behind the second layer) → 15. | └─ ESSENTIAL KIT (module-first dock)                  |    **5** | `ESSENTIAL_KIT_SLOTS` in `src/effects/essentialKit.ts` — EQ / DYNAMICS (`compressor`) / SATURATION / DELAY / REVERB, 3 factory characters each, one `LOAD KIT` click inserts all five in signal-flow order as a SINGLE undo step (`applyEssentialKit`). Slots hold presets, never chain ids: a single device's measured factory trim is the whole compensation, so no chain-level trim can double it. Complementary to the SOURCE-first dock (`SOURCE_PROFILES`), which starts from what you record (kick/808/vocal) rather than which device does what. Locked by `tests/effects-essential-kit.test.ts` (24/24) and a real-browser gate (`node scripts/verify-essential-kit.mjs`, 33/33 in Chromium: the dock renders in the DEV dock panel, LOAD KIT inserts the five devices in signal-flow order, the chain grows by exactly five, the fifteen daily devices are the first fifteen the ADD FX grid offers while all 57 stay insertable exactly once, a curve drag and a knob drag each commit to the document as one undo step, and one Ctrl+Z removes the whole kit).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| └─ kit multiview (five devices, live)                 |    **5** | `src/ui/KitMultiview.tsx` renders every kit device actually on the track side by side under the slot picker, each on its own curated `primaryParamIds` MAIN page — the same page the single-device editor shows as MAIN, so a knob means the same thing in both places. `EDITOR_OVERRIDES` gained saturation / delay / reverb first pages (EQ and Compressor already had one), so each kit slot shows exactly four knobs; a slot without a curated page would render as a column with nothing to tweak rather than an error. It is a VIEW of the document, not a second source of truth — values read from `track.effects[i].params`, every drag commits through `setEffectParam`, which is why undo restores the stored value rather than the rendered one (verified live: reverb DECAY `4.20 s → 15.02 s` → Ctrl+Z → `4.20 s`). Two facts the spec pins rather than assumes: **EQ declares no `mix` at all** (a wet-on-wet filter — it is bypassed, not faded), and **Compressor keeps `mix` off its MAIN page**, reaching it through the shared MIX/FEEDBACK/SYNC quick-control strip. The EQ column additionally paints a **response curve** (`src/effects/eqCurve.ts` + `src/ui/EqCurveCanvas.tsx`) instead of four bare sliders. `eqCurve` is an OFFLINE transcription of the worklet's own arithmetic, not `BiquadFilterNode`: `eq-processor.js` deliberately uses PARALLEL shelves and a bilinear Q pre-correction so both shelf ends land exactly on 0 dB and +/-G, and a BiquadFilter response would draw a curve that is close to, but not, the one the track is processing. That would be worse than no curve. The curve is DRAGGABLE: each point is one of the four `primaryParamIds` gains, so a point and the knob beneath it are literally the same control - dragging either moves the other. **Two measured defects had to be fixed before the gesture worked at all, and both were invisible to the unit tests** (63/63 green while the curve was unusable): (1) the DEV panel is 251px tall against 756px of content, and `footer.statusbar` sits LATER in the DOM than the canvas, so it won the overlap - `elementsFromPoint` at the curve's centre returned `FOOTER.statusbar`, not the canvas, and the Compressor/Saturation/Delay/Reverb knobs at the same height were unreachable too. The panel now scrolls as one column and `.kit-multiview` no longer nests a second scrollbar inside an already-clipped parent. **That scroll is not this module's debt.** Attributed by injecting candidate rules at runtime and reading `scrollHeight - clientHeight` for each (2026-10-16): 505px over as shipped; 299px if `.device-surface` may shrink again (-206px); 171px if `.source-macro-dock` is hidden too (-128px); 0 only if the multiview is hidden as well. `.device-surface` carries `flex: 1 0 220px; min-height: 220px` from `42-device-slot-finishing.css:3`, added the same day, and its own comment states "the Devices panel owns the resulting vertical scroll" - a comment that was FALSE against `overflow: hidden`, leaving 206px of FX editor with nowhere to go. The panel scroll is what makes it true. Letting the editor shrink to zero instead is exactly what its 220px floor exists to prevent. The kit dock was compacted anyway (324px -> 281px, curve 46px -> 34px, knob row 34px -> 27px), because less scrolling is better than more, but compaction alone cannot make a 251px panel hold a 756px column: at 281px the dock is still larger than the entire panel. That means "scroll to the control" and "see the whole dock" are DIFFERENT claims - at 281px in a 251px viewport the dock is fully visible at no scroll offset at all - and the gate only asserts the first, by scrolling THE TARGET into view (`block: "nearest"`). An earlier version scrolled to the panel's bottom instead, which is wrong because the kit dock is not the panel's last block (`device-surface` is) and that put the dock out through the top, so the probe measured whatever lay behind it. (2) This gate's own HIGH MID probe pointed at 56.6% of the curve width, which is where 1100 Hz lands, not the 2500 Hz band (69.9%); nothing was under the pointer, the drag never began, and because the following Ctrl+Z then undid the LOAD KIT instead of the never-made gesture, the whole kit vanished and six later checks failed as collateral - a wrong constant does not fail quietly once, it poisons every check that assumes the step before it worked. Hit-testing (`hitEqBand`) compares the pointer against each band's OWN response in isolation, not the cascade: two neighbouring bands overlap, so the sum would always look nearest to something. A band is only grabbable inside a radius in BOTH axes, so matching gain at a distant frequency is not a match. The document is written ONCE on pointerup, never per frame — one undo step per gesture, the same settle `Slider` uses, with the engine gliding the parameter between. Mid-drag the canvas paints a local value and the live knob still reads the stored one; release commits from a ref, not the render closure, because React 18 flushes pointermove from a macrotask and a fast flick can release before the last move renders. A press that never moved writes nothing - and the first implementation of that rule was wrong in the way only a real gesture could catch. It raised the "the user moved" flag in `onPointerDown`, from the pointer's POSITION relative to the band rather than from pointer movement, so a press-and-release anywhere inside the grab radius committed whatever dB the pointer happened to sit at (measured: -1.09 dB from a click the user never made), and then swallowed the undo step the kit test needed. `tests/effects-eq-curve-drag.test.ts` was 16/16 GREEN throughout, because all 16 tests exercise pure functions in `eqCurve.ts` and the defect was never in one - it was in the component's event handling. `tests/eq-curve-gesture.test.tsx` (12 tests) drives the real component with real pointer events and is the regression lock; falsified against the old code it turns exactly the two click tests red and leaves the other ten green. `tests/effects-eq-curve-drag.test.ts` (16/16) covers the hit-test and the axis mapping; the gate drags HIGH MID on a live curve. Locked by `tests/effects-eq-curve.test.ts` (15/15, asserting the physics: flat passband, 3 dB at each HP/LP corner, a +12 dB shelf that does NOT droop to +7 at 20 kHz) + `tests/effects-essential-kit.test.ts` (24/24) + `scripts/verify-essential-kit.mjs` (18/18 Chromium, run via `npx vite-node` so the DOM menu is compared against the live `EFFECT_META` count rather than a literal). | **Vitest spec files**                                 | **1173** | `tests/` files matching `*.test.ts` and `*.test.tsx`, excluding `tests/e2e/`; re-measured 2026-10-23 as `git ls-files` (committed files only, so parallel sessions cannot flap the gate); the 2026-10-21 meter-disclosure wave adds `tests/adr0057-meter-disclosure.test.ts` (24 specs: the metre is never changed SILENTLY — the command, the intent read-back, the MCP response and the AI generation path all disclose what the new metre MEASURES and what happens to the material, they all say the SAME words, and the disclosure fires when the SIGNATURE changes (4/4 → 2/2 keeps the bar but moves the click), not when the bar changes; the guard finds every site that builds a metre, and the ledger may only SHRINK); the 2026-10-21 hygiene wave adds `tests/format-scope.test.ts` (4 specs: the format gate's SCOPE is an invariant - `format:check` named only `src` and `tests` for months, and 191 prettier-dirty files piled up in the trees it never looked at; the guard pins every maintained source tree, the repository's root documents, the exclusion of generated trees, and the symmetry of the write variant, and it is proven able to go red); the 2026-10-21 bootstrap-diet wave adds `tests/boot-path-seams.test.ts` (3 specs: the studio boot path must keep the heavy modules OUT of its static closure - the AI engine, the mix planner, the intent routing table, the render manifest, the zip writer, the MIDI writers, the hum panel and the checkpoint reader - so the 2200 KB boot-path budget cannot be undone by one careless import; the guard walks the same source graph the budget script measures at chunk level, in 6 ms instead of a 2.5-minute build); the 2026-10-21 plugin-audit and preset-loudness waves add `tests/plugin-audit-materials.test.ts` (11 specs: the host probe's MATERIAL rule — the drum bus first, the melodic tracks, and the SPREAD kit LAST; the rule lives in a Node-pure module so it is unit-testable without a browser, and the old mono-only rule is run through the same predicate as a negative control) and `tests/preset-loudness-path.test.ts` (4 specs: the loudness map and its drift gate must measure the SAME synthesis — the worklet-path list has one owner, read by both consumers — with a negative control per structural contract); the 2026-10-21 reproducibility wave adds `tests/plugin-audit-determinism.test.ts` (4 specs: the audit's document and every measurement unit mint ids DETERMINISTICALLY — two house templates built in deterministic mode carry identical ids, with the negative control that without the mode they differ, plus five structural contracts on the audit's own wiring — the document's base, each unit's base, the two bases 1000 apart, the scope restored in a `finally`, and every unit running its work INSIDE the scope — each with a mutation that has to break it); the 2026-10-20 audit adds `tests/vitest-collection-scope.test.ts` (7 specs: the run collects KYX's own specs only - every foreign tree in the project root (a co-tenant workspace, a backup dir, a JUNCTION into `node_modules`) must be covered by the vitest `exclude` list, with the matcher and the guard itself proven able to fail) and `tests/adr0057-metrical-step-grid.test.ts` (21 specs: the step grid's bar is the document's metrical bar - the truth table, the 4/4 negative control, a whole-bar pattern never drifting in 3/4, 6/8, 4/2 or 7/8, and a guard that the steps-per-bar formula has exactly one owner and that no UI or MCP file converts a step count to a bar count by hand). ADR 0057 Stage 2a adds `tests/adr0057-metrical-intent-length.test.ts` (15 specs: the intent engine asks for length in BARS and the document's meter answers in steps - the default length is one bar of that meter, a bar-framed request is resolved by that meter, a step-framed request stays a step count, the 4/4 negative control (the same spec, the same intent hash, the same beat), the plan generates a whole number of the document's bars, the brief carries a bar count beside a step count, the MCP surface takes any whole number of the document's bars and reads the length back in both units - and a second guard that the intent/UI/MCP layer may not re-derive the bar by hand, with a synthetic violation proving the guard can fail). ADR 0056 Stage 2 adds `tests/adr0056-stage2-bar-unit.test.ts` (4 specs: a 6/8 clip lands on the musical bar, the render length and the bar's audible duration follow the meter, and the count-in accent measures the document's bar - each with a 4/4 negative control, and both mutations falsified). ADR 0056 Stage 2 then REPLACED the arrangement-grid disclosure spec with `tests/adr0056-pattern-grid-warning.test.tsx` (7 specs: the PATTERN grid's truth table - one pattern per bar exactly for 4/4, 2/2 and 8/8 - the 6/8 warning text, and the 4/4 negative control) plus `tests/adr0056-pattern-grid.test.ts` (the measured pattern-grid drift in 6/8 - the phase at successive bar lines is 0, 1440, 960, 480, so a groove's downbeat reaches the bar line only every fourth bar - with the 4/4 negative control). ADR 0056's second cut adds `tests/adr0056-grid-warning-disclosure.test.tsx` (6 specs: the 4/4 grid has to be disclosed BEFORE the user commits to the meter, not only once files have been inspected - the meter control is live from the moment the dialog opens, so gating the warning on the inspection disclosed the mismatch only after the commitment; with the 4/4, 2/2 and 8/8 negative controls, the invalid-meter case, and the import-still-blocked check). Concurrent sessions' `tests/doc-delta-order-replay.test.ts`, `tests/intent-decline-ux.test.ts`, `tests/intent-degree.test.ts`, `tests/clip-clipboard-undo-fidelity.test.ts` and `tests/command-delta-family-guard.test.ts` also landed in the same window, which is why the live count moved past this ADR's own file. the 2026-10-11 `rg --files` measurement read 959 and predates the dusty-jazz family (adds `tests/dusty-jazz-family.test.ts`), the wow &amp; flutter wave and the concurrent SK-vocab/tsar waves with `rg --files tests`, excluding `tests/e2e/`; the 940-file live count also includes `tests/portal-presets.test.ts`, `tests/rezonans-pitchtrack.test.ts`, `tests/rezonans-soak.test.ts`, and the in-flight `tests/ui/SceneStrip.test.tsx`. The editor lock/clipboard wave adds `tests/clip-lock-verbs.test.ts` (9 specs: every verb the lock contract names refuses a locked clip, with the primitive-verb and unlocked-clip controls), `tests/clip-clipboard-integrity.test.ts` (11 specs: deep-clone payload, one-transaction paste/undo, repeated paste independence, dead-track fallback, dead-scene refusal, save/load survival) and `tests/ui/clip-lock-block-drag.test.tsx` (6 specs: scene and audio block drag, refusal costs no undo entry, unlock restores the gesture). `tests/ui/drag-zoom-invariance.test.tsx` (3 specs) pins that a live drag keeps the scale it captured at pointerdown, so a mid-gesture Ctrl+wheel zoom cannot re-scale the gesture. `tests/ui/malformed-clip-render.test.tsx` (8 specs) pins that a non-finite or absurd clip position cannot blank or crash the timeline: the grid extent drops non-finite contributions and bounds the pre-measurement fallback that previously threw `RangeError: Invalid array length`. `tests/clip-sanitization-boundary.test.ts` (8 specs) pins which malformed project values the store boundary drops (non-finite position, non-positive duration) and which it lets through (an absurd but finite position). `tests/ui/mount-unmount-balance.test.tsx` (4 specs) measures that window/document listeners and intervals registered by the arrangement panel are released on unmount, including mid-gesture. The wow &amp; flutter wave adds `tests/wow-flutter.test.ts` (23 specs: the cents->amplitude conversion is checked as pure math and the DSP is then driven headlessly with isolated impulses, recovering each impulse delay by amplitude centroid because a linear interpolant splits a unit impulse into exactly `[1-f, f]`, and the pitch deviation follows analytically from `dr/dt = 1 - dD/dt`; depth 0 is exactly transparent, depth N delivers N cents at the requested rate, drift is a bounded wander, the worst corner never wraps the read, both channels stay bit-identical and drift is seed-deterministic). That measurement found the depth control delivering **0.02 cents for a requested 10** - a factor of 500 - because the conversion multiplied by the base delay (the granular resampling law `dD/dt / D`, which does not apply to a continuously modulated delay line) and the read offset halved the wobble on top of it. The browser half is `tests/e2e/26-wow-flutter-measurement.spec.ts` (7 specs, same method against the shipped bundle), and `scripts/probe-wow-flutter.mjs` is the standalone diagnostic that produced the table. The historical detail below records earlier additions; the live count is the authoritative value. The count includes concurrent sessions' in-flight spec files, the audio-engine audit's `tests/fx-pattern-sync.test.ts` + `tests/audio-engine-audit12-lifecycle-pins.test.ts`, the MCP wire wave's `tests/mcp-sdk-client.test.ts` (5 specs: official MCP SDK client legacy handshake, audio content blocks, modern `server/discover` + `_meta` + `-32022`, header-era requests, structured pass-through + missing-structured flag), the lazy-factory-bank fix's `tests/mcp-preset-bank-warm.test.ts` (5 specs: cold-bank start pin, `loadPreset`/`listPresets` from a cold bank, honest unknown-preset, mixer preset-NAME read-back), the UX-polish wave's `tests/ui/NumericDraftInput.test.tsx` (7 specs: one commit on blur/Enter, Escape revert, cleared-field cancel, non-finite ignored, idempotent Enter) + `tests/ui/TextPromptDialog.test.tsx` (7 specs: trimmed submit, empty refused, Escape/backdrop cancel, Enter vs Ctrl+Enter, error slot, draft re-seed), the smart-comping wave's `tests/smart-comp-scoring.test.ts` (8 known-answer specs on synthetic PCM), `tests/smart-comp-plan.test.ts` (9 specs: per-bar winner, seam merging, uncovered-bar honesty, undecoded-PCM fallback, determinism, one-command apply/undo) + `tests/ui/ArrangementPanel.smart-comp.test.tsx` (3 specs: evidence card, CANCEL never dispatches, APPLY is exactly one command) + `tests/comp-core.test.ts` (7 specs pinning the shared planning spine both comping lanes delegate to: earlier-take ties, coverage-beats-score, honest holes, span merge, null winner) and a concurrent session's `tests/gallery-battles.test.ts` and the Session Theatre's `tests/producer-theatre.test.ts` (re-measured again 2026-10-04 via `find tests -name "*.test.ts*" \| grep -v /e2e/ \| wc -l`). +1 for the dock chrome wave's `tests/ui/DockChrome.test.tsx` (5 specs pinning the always-mounted dock tab row — ROADMAP-UI-2027 V1). Re-measured 803 with the concurrent waves; includes the FL-interactions wave's `tests/ui/fl-interactions.test.tsx` (8 specs: wheel-adjust bursts settle as ONE commit, Ctrl fine step, disabled/horizontal-wheel inertness, DragNumber round-trip no-op, live statusbar hint — ROADMAP-UI-2027 V3). +browser-strip wave's `tests/ui/browser-strip.test.tsx` (5 specs: lazy mount, collapse/expand persistence, pad + instrument assign routing, drag payload MIME/fallback — ROADMAP-UI-2027 V4). The Studio I/O wave adds `tests/studio-io.test.ts` (6: ASIO IPC bridge tiers + boundary, output-device persist/setSinkId honesty, default-first device listing) and `tests/ui/io-panel.test.tsx` (5: honest degradation without setSinkId/desktop bridge, input persist, disabled test tone, ASIO section via bridge). The F4 gallery-flywheel wave adds `tests/gallery-family-tree.test.ts` (5 specs: full-tree collection with newest-first DFS, legacy gallery-parent backfill join, mixed-era chains, orphan/cycle guards, unpublished-parent honesty). +1 for the suite-expectations ratchet's `tests/suite-expectations.test.ts` (16 specs: report parsing incl. collection-error pseudo-ids, both ratchet directions, flaky exemption, TODO-owner guard, bless metadata preservation, CLI exit-code contract). +1 for the honest-gate sweep's `tests/honest-gates.test.ts` (17 specs after wave 2: shared envelope-crest floor, F1 rhythm lane stationary-null contract, estimateKey tonal-evidence floors incl. colored-noise robustness, bare-sine root honesty; plus DC-free section-mix measurement with a positive control, degenerate take-family comp determinism, level-match honest degradation and clamp surfacing). +1 for the ZENIT/M2 param audit's `tests/zenit-m2-param-audit.test.ts` (12 specs: all 9 ZENIT macros at both extremes -> exact stage writes, ceiling<->limit coupling incl. load-order independence, def<->processor descriptor range equality, 23-param min/max aliveness with designed excitations - found the APEKS release dead knob, fixed 308c15f9). nothing; the spec re-implements the legacy formula and compares it against the production one, so the 4/4 identity cannot be satisfied by the code agreeing with itself) ; the 2026-10-22 worker imports are declared for the optimizer up front, the sibling's zod is excluded rather than included so dev runs the same zod as production, the scan stays pinned to the app entry, and both configs declare the react dedupe themselves - the resolved config cannot be asserted on because mergeConfig concatenates the plugin's list, which is a false green) |

Real Chromium registers the provider ready (~1,200 ms cold load, ~29 ms smoke inference). Private assistant-authored 144-row EN diagnostic: combined 71/72 actions, 36/36 clarifications, 36/36 safe abstentions, zero false-positive actions. Synthetic/regression scores do not establish independent generalization or musical quality. Desktop Ollama/SFT is a separate runtime; see `docs/LOCAL-INTENT-MODEL-SFT-RUNBOOK.md`. **SFT LLM LIVE + ACTIVE BY DEFAULT**: LFM2.5-1.2B-Instruct LoRA cez Ollama (`kyx-intent-v30-q8`, Q8_0 — recipe wave LoRA r32/α64/4ep na 1906 párov; **95.6 % attempted-exact / wrongKind 0 / abstain 1 na 272-row val**, rovnaké-alebo-Lepšie než vlastné f16 94.5 %/1/0 pri POLOVICI footprintu 1.16 vs 2.18 GB a ~150 ms/generáciu — f16 bojovalo o VRAM na 6 GB karte). ## Flagship plugin implementations

| Plugin suite    | Brand        | DSP core / ownership                                         | Worklet bundle                     |
| --------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------- |
| `fxeq`          | **PRISM**    | `src/effects/fxeq-core/` (vendored upstream)                 | `public/fxeq-worklet.js`           |
| `ultina`        | **VLYX**     | `src/effects/ultina-core/` (vendored upstream)               | `public/ultina-worklet.js`         |
| `ozvena`        | **DRIFT**    | `src/effects/ozvena-core/` (vendored upstream)               | `public/ozvena-worklet.js`         |
| `kaskada`       | **RIFT**     | first-party DSP in `src/audio-worklets/kaskada-processor.js` | `public/core-worklet.js`           |
| `morphdynamics` | **MORPH**    | first-party DSP in `src/effects/morph-dynamics-core/`        | `public/morph-dynamics-worklet.js` |
| `rezonans`      | **REZONANS** | first-party DSP in `src/effects/rezonans-core/`              | `public/rezonans-worklet.js`       |

All five flagship suites use AudioWorklet DSP. PRISM, VLYX and DRIFT include separately vendored cores reconciled by `scripts/vendor-*.mjs`; RIFT and MORPH are first-party DSP owned in-tree. Plugin-specific regression / golden test suites live under `tests/<plugin>-vectors/` and `tests/<plugin>-golden/`.

### Artist roster size

**658 artist presets** in `src/intent/artists.ts` (`ARTIST_PRESETS.length`). Genre spread: house 129 · trap 122 · dnb 66 · techno 60 · boombap 43 · ambient 39 · phonk 27 · drill 26 · ukg 23 · drone 21 · jersey 20 · detroit 20 · amapiano 19 · trance 10 · postrock 8 · hyperpop 7 · chiptune 6 · eurodance 6 · latin 6. (House was 66 before its depth wave, techno 24, ambient 37, ukg 14 — the genres with the biggest roster holes relative to their weight; the three-family + drone + postrock waves added the latest 36.)

**Every artist preset routes to a first-class groove — locked by a standing gate.** The groove-enrichment waves (commits `2368e6c4` wave 3, `362c11e` wave 4, `bddba506` wave 5, `grooves-wave-6` wave 6) closed every remaining "closest-fit" mapping: each sub-genre that previously borrowed a neighbouring groove now owns one with a researched tempo pocket and its own pad signature. Highlights — `house.basshouse` (Fisher/ACRAZE skip-bass), `house.ghouse` (Don Diablo French deep), `house.footwork` (RP Boo polyrhythmic, straight-grid), `house.baile` (Anitta tambor on the offbeat), `house.kpop` (BTS half-time snare flip), `house.reggaeton` (J Balvin one-drop tick), `house.melodic` (Lane 8 rolling ride), `techno.psytrance` (Astrix rolling TB-303), `techno.ebm` (Surgeon mechanical tick), `techno.hardstyle` (Headhunterz reverse-bass), `ambient.citypop` (Anri rim-shot backbeat), `ambient.synthwave` (Kavinsky gated snare), `trap.corridos` (Peso Pluma march backbeat), `trap.dancehall` (Sean Paul one-drop), `trap.bedroom` (Joji rim tap), `trap.trapsoul` (Bryson Tiller limping kick), `hyperpop.decon` (A.G. Cook snare-roll glitch). Wave 6 added the last five families the engine could not express — `techno.gabber` (Angerfist 160–180 stomp), `ambient.triphop` (Bonobo dusty halftime), `house.reggae` (Bob Marley one-drop, empty beat 1), `trap.bassdubstep` (Skrillex/Excision drop-era), `house.shoegaze` (buried wall-of-guitars drums) — plus the **zero-dangling gate**: every `ARTIST_PRESETS` entry's `genre.style` must resolve through `getGrooveById` (a mismatch was a silent random-groove fallback). Locked by `tests/grooves-wave-{3,4,5,6}.test.ts` + the "no artist preset may dangle" describe block in `tests/intent-artists.test.ts` (registration + `resolveGroove` + 16-step row shape + swing clamp + lane signatures + full `generateLocalResult` smoke + the fixed 44-dim ONNX prior contract). The gap map is `docs/VOCABULARY-GAP-RESEARCH.md`.

| `symbolic-melodic-v1.onnx` | ~18 KB | `melodic-features.v1` (29 features) | melodic next-note prior, **preferred** (honest valDegreeAcc 0.6897 / valDurationAcc 0.8621 on rows it never saw)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `symbolic-melodic-v2.onnx` | ~21 KB | `melodic-features-v2` (41 features) | melodic embedding variant, **retrained 2026-09-27 (ds.v3, style-aware conditioning)** (honest degree 0.4828 → **0.5946**, duration 0.3793 → **0.5766**; `weightPower: 0.5` in the manifest)                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `symbolic-melodic-v3.onnx` | ~28 KB | `melodic-features-v3` (68 features) | harmony-aware W1 candidate (chord root/quality/function + next-chord + motif embedding; ds.v3 9 592 samples, valDegreeAcc 0.5699 / valDurationAcc 0.5) — **artifacts + worker runner (`runMelodicNextV3`) ready, NOT wired into the provider** (active melodic chain stays conditioning→v2, else v1); parked after the W2 MIDI-corpus gate FAIL (2026-10-04) as the chord-aware training line |

All seven have lazy worker loaders with bounded timeouts + circuit breaker + deterministic heuristic fallback (`src/ai/ranking/ranker-client.ts`, `src/ai/symbolic/prior-client.ts`); the provider's **active** chain is drums v3 and melodic v2/v1 (v3 melodic is parked, see the table above). Inference never runs on the audio thread. Two further models are lazy-fetched on demand (not in git): multilingual MiniLM q8 ~118 MB (`npm run semantic:fetch` → `public/models/semantic/`) and AST AudioSet q8 ~86.6 MB (`npm run audio:fetch` → `public/models/audio/`); both degrade to keyword/heuristic paths when absent. The retrained `hybrid v3` symbolic prior (label smoothing + variant embeddings, logit saturation fix) is the active generation source behind the candidate bank; see `INTENT_ENGINE.md` for the full conditioning chain (semantic embedding, user style vector, SUNO MODE button).

**Melodic prior audit + fix (2026-09-27)** — `docs/MELODIC-PRIOR-AUDIT-2026-09-27.md`. Read-only analysis (`npm run audit:melodic`, `npm run audit:melodic:weights`) plus a pre-registered retrain gate (`python scripts/gate-melodic-retrain.py`, trains a fresh model per fold). Findings: (1) the v1/v2 comparison was never apples-to-apples — v1 trained on 190 samples, v2 on 239; (2) the shipped 15 % split validates on 29 rows / 4 groups (95 % CI ≈ ±18 pp); (3) **`train-symbolic-melodic.py` multiplied the duration gradient by `(wt / n)` twice**, so the head optimised `wt²` (rare-class pull up to 10.2×) and never descended the loss it printed — finite-difference proof: analytic 0.0224 vs true 0.1463; (4) the first pass of this audit also leaked (it evaluated shipped models on splits they had trained on — `audit-melodic-leakage-check.py` shows v2 degree 0.7952 seen vs 0.4828 unseen), so only gate numbers are quoted here; (5) **the one-hot v1 recipe cannot represent dnb** — `melodicGenreOf()` maps it to house, so all 49 dnb rows carry the house one-hot block (`audit-melodic-dnb-onehot.py`) and the runtime conditions dnb as house on the v1 path; the embedding v2 is the only recipe with a dnb centroid. **Fix applied:** delete the double multiply, `--weight-power 0.5` (sqrt tempering, ratio 10.2× → 3.2×), shared trainer primitives in `train_symbolic_melodic_lib.py`; v2 retrained and validated.

**Melodic depth wave (2026-09-27, ds.v3)** — the audit's remaining data gaps are closed for dnb. `MELODIC_BY_STYLE` dialects (dnb.techstep/ragga/sambass/halftime/crossbreed/minimal + jungle, and the earlier amapiano/metal/dembow/… set) now reach BOTH generators: `generate-symbolic-melodic-dataset.mts` emits `symbolic-melodic-ds.v3` at **861 rows / 119 groups** (was 239/33), and `generate-augmented-data.mts` walks the dialect maps too (**12 672 augmented rows**, of which dnb 3 784 — dialect groups 576; dnb was 0 before). The embedding conditioning is now **style-aware** in one shared `SemanticLookup` (trainer + gate + all audit scripts): a dialect group (`dnb.techstep#…`) resolves to that style's vector, a genre group (`dnb#…`) to the genre centroid; a missing prefix **fails loudly** instead of silently zero-filling (the zero-fill was why the gate died with "genre outside the vocab" the moment dialect rows entered the dataset). Retrained v2 (gate 5-fold PASS): honest degree **0.5354**, duration **0.5621**; shipped-split degree **0.5766**, duration **0.5676** (was 0.5517/0.5517); manifest reports valDegreeAcc 0.5946 / valDurationAcc 0.5766, sha `c00cfb972990b61e`. The augmentation writer is now atomic-with-retry (`write-temp + rename`) so a transient Windows lock on the 260 MB drum dataset can no longer kill a regeneration.

**MIDI corpus W2 (2026-10-04) — pipeline ODHADNUTÝ, `--gate FAIL`** — `docs/W2-MIDI-CORPUS-AUDIT-2026-10-04.md`. Public-domain MIDI corpus added to the melodic prior's _training_ path (train-only, validation stays library-pure). **New:** `scripts/fetch-midi-corpus.mjs` (replayable, only `license === "Public Domain"`, SHA-1 verified per piece), `scripts/ingest-midi-corpus.mts` + pure core `src/ai/midi-corpus-ingest.ts` (Krumhansl key detect from MIDI chroma, conservative 3-role split, 16-step quantize, degree inversion, wrap-around), `scripts/append-classical-embeddings.mts` (adds `classical.{baroque,classical,romantic,impressionist,modern}` to `style-embeddings.json`, 194→199 styles), `--midi-corpus` flag on `train-symbolic-melodic.py` + `gate-melodic-retrain.py` (shared merge code), `tests/midi-corpus-ingest.test.ts` (20). **Corpus: 345 PD pieces → 29 730 next-note rows (338 ingested).** Closes the ds.v3 data gaps: **d1 0→3 491, d5 0→2 935, duration-8 54→736** rows. **Gate verdict: FAIL (degree 0.5447 → 0.4774 pooled, −0.067 on the electronic library val)** — consistent across 2/3-fold, both conditioning regions (`classical.*` and `ambient`) and all role subsets. Classical counterpoint's stepwise/legato distribution over-trains against the electronic syncopated bank. **Decision: NOT shipped in the melodic prior**; the corpus + pipeline are the training base for the planned W1 chord-aware model. Shipped `symbolic-melodic-v2` is unchanged (opt-in flag only).

**Personal prior trainer W3 (2026-10-04, jadro)** — in-app personalization základ: `src/intent/personal-melodic-trainer.ts` (čistý TS port `train_symbolic_melodic_lib.py` — Adam, class-weighted CE na oboch hlavách, duration hlava vážená **presne raz**; **fine-tune zo shipnutých váh, nie od nuly**, takže na osobnej ceste nie je žiadny RNG) + `src/intent/personal-melodic-onnx.ts` (čítač ONNX inicializátorov bez dependency + JSON payload pre IndexedDB). 19/19 testov zelené vrátane **finite-difference gradient checku** (rovnaký dôkaz ako audit 2026-09-27 na double-weight bug) a ONNX reader reprodukcie Gemm matematiky na reálnom artefakte. `tests/personal-melodic-trainer.test.ts` (11) + `tests/personal-melodic-onnx.test.ts` (8).

**Producer DNA 2.0 / features.v2 (2026-10-04, HOTOVÉ)** — W4. `src/ai/features/pattern-features-v2.ts`: **aditívny** kontrakt (prvých 54 dimenzií byte-identických s v1, +15 meraní: bass noteDensity/rootAlignment/movement/syncopation/registerStability · harmony voicingMovement/harmonicRhythm/voiceRichness/chordDensity · arrangement roleCount/registerSpread/rhythmicAlignment · 3 per-role presence flags). Roly riešené generátorovým resolverom `instrumentTrackForRole`. **Dual-read**: ledger validuje oba kontrakty podľa deklarovanej verzie, v1 vektory sa dopĺňajú na v2 neutrálnym 0,5 (nulový rozdiel na nových osiach — legacy hlasovanie nemôže „naučiť" basu/harmóniu, ktorú nemeralo). **bass/harmony** preference adaptéry už nie sú `null`; taste-probe picker ich navrhuje. **Proaktívne probe** (`src/intent/proactive-probe.ts`): po 3 generáciách bez hlasovania panel sám ponúkne jednu kontrolovanú otázku (hlasovanie aj odmietnutie resetujú sériu). Nový store `STORE_PERSONAL_MODELS` (DB v13). 70/70 + 46/46 testov.

**Recursive Producer DNA (2026-10-07; in progress; ADR 0026–0032)** — bounded local memory, pause/export/import/forget, resumable lineage, explicit intent corrections, and pairwise personal ranking feed the existing intent pipeline. New render snapshots use `audio.v2` (level prefix plus timbre/periodicity and optional stereo summaries); imported `audio.v1` vectors remain valid and missing dimensions remain unavailable. Section comparisons use a separate `section` task and enable votes only after both complete pattern-plus-FX renders finish playback. Song comparisons use their own task; after at least two complete song lanes have rendered, the UI can re-rank those lanes with symbolic, audio, and `song-dramaturgy.v1` evidence under a combined ±0.20 residual cap. The fixed 33-value dramaturgy vector represents section order/form, energy/density/complexity arcs, instrumentation turnover, transitions, pattern contrast, and section-derived melodic/harmony features. Song A/B can label overall impression, development, transitions, contrast, harmony, sound, or mix; a separate context-weighted ranker learns structural focuses, while sound/mix labels train audio preferences. The leakage-safe evaluator reports song dramaturgy accuracy/lift separately and excludes audio-focus labels from that metric. A/B observations can preserve each candidate's effective personal score at feedback time; symbolic/audio and audio.v1/v2 paired metrics share the same holdout. An opt-in blind pilot arms the next pattern generation before it is shown, compares distinct top picks from one shared bank with cryptographically randomized sides, hides the ranked bank and lane receipt through the response, and records display assignments for pilot-only score and side-bias audits. Pilot comparisons measure selector ranking over that shared bank, not the candidate generator itself. Probe selection combines measured axis isolation with reason-model predictive entropy and evidence shrinkage; this is a query-value heuristic, not calibrated expected information gain. Initial build ranking remains bounded to symbolic/form evidence, and lane rendering stays user-selected. Raw PCM is not stored. Grouped holdout validation of the new dramaturgy contract and the real-user pilot outcome remain open.

**Personal prior W3 (2026-10-04, HOTOVÉ)** — in-app personalizácia „Nauč sa ma": `src/intent/personal-melodic-trainer.ts` (čistý TS port `train_symbolic_melodic_lib.py`; **fine-tune zo shipnutých váh, nie od nuly** — bez RNG, regresia vylúčená konštrukciou), `src/intent/personal-melodic-onnx.ts` (ONNX inicializátory bez dependency + JSON payload), `src/intent/personal-melodic-inference.ts` (plain-JS forward pass so zhodou na shipped logits), `src/persistence/PersonalModelRepository.ts` (nový `STORE_PERSONAL_MODELS`, DB v13, kľúč `kind#baseModelHash`, prune sirotkov), worker overlay (`prior-types/worker/client` — personal > shipped, fallback vždy, flag `pf:personal-prior`), `src/intent/personal-melodic-flow.ts` + `IntentPanel` 🧠 NAUČ SA MA (ledger → fine-tune → A/B dôkaz vs shipped → inštalácia len nad 70 %; ranker preferenčné skupiny pre offline retrain), `src/intent/semantic.ts` korpus rastie z ★ (D7). 64/64 testov v 7 súboroch vrátane finite-difference gradient checku.

**DnB retrain (2026-09-26)** — the DnB vocabulary wave extended the drum dataset from `symbolic-prior-ds.v1` (87 552 samples / 33 grooves) to **`symbolic-prior-ds.v2` (95 232 samples / 64 grooves)** by training all seven `dnb.*` grooves through the semantic pack (`style-embeddings.json` gained the dnb centroids + variants; the frozen one-hot `PRIOR_STYLE_VOCAB` is untouched). All three embedding-conditioned artifacts were retrained: **v3 valAUC 0.9203 → 0.9047** overall — but on the _identical old split_ (ds.v1 rows reproduced byte-for-byte) the new model scores **0.9247 vs 0.9163**, i.e. the drop is dilution by the hard new dnb groups, not a regression; per-group, `dnb.roller` rose **0.657 → 0.849** and old groups moved ≤ ±2 %. v2 rose **0.8805 → 0.8883** (old split), melodic-v2 rose **0.429 → 0.483** (239 rows / 33 groups, now training DnB bass/chord/lead references added to `MELODIC_BY_GENRE`). Runtime routes `dnb.*` styles past v3's one-hot gate straight to the v2 semantic branch. Full gate: `validate-symbolic-prior v2/v3`, `validate-symbolic-melodic v2`, `v3 hybrid gate 3/3 PASS`, `smoke-symbolic-prior 10/10`.

## Platform reach

- **Browser** — Chromium-family, Firefox and Microsoft Edge are target environments. The current revision passes the Chromium browser verifier at 260/260; Firefox/Edge cross-browser coverage and manual Safari/iOS Safari checks remain separate gates.
- **Desktop** — Windows x64 shipped (NSIS installer + portable exe via `electron-builder`). The MRT2 macOS arm64 path now has a GitHub Actions package gate: after all shared CI checks pass, it builds a DMG + ZIP, verifies the DMG and packaged arm64 app/helper, boots the packaged Electron app in smoke mode, then uploads a 14-day QA artifact. This artifact is unsigned/unnotarized and does not include model weights; it is not a public macOS release or proof of MRT2 inference. Windows MRT2 uses a separate optional companion boundary and remains capture/near-realtime/realtime only after capability and benchmark evidence. Auto-update through GitHub Releases (ADR 0011).
- **Android / KYX Mobile** — PWA-first runtime profile (ADR 0051). v1 is sketching, arrangement and mixing with a capability-based touch profile; Play Store packaging is a TWA generated by `npm run mobile:twa`. A Capacitor/WebView shell is optional (ADR 0052); the `window.kyxNative` bridge covers USB/BLE MIDI, SAF, foreground service and audio focus with PWA fallbacks. A native Oboe/C++ audio host is not built: Phase 3 is gated by exported acoustic latency reports (ADR 0053, `docs/MOBILE-LATENCY.md`); reported `baseLatency`/`outputLatency` alone are not evidence. The PWA install precaches only the app shell (~9 MB); samples are runtime-cached with explicit offline packs, and worklet URLs are content-hashed via `public/worklets-manifest.json`. Web MIDI without the shell, guaranteed background audio and low-latency tracking remain explicit non-goals until the evidence/shell/native-engine work is scheduled. Real-device latency and install measurements remain an owner gate.
- **Safari / iOS Safari** — manual smoke only; not covered by automated browser verifier.
- **macOS / Linux desktop** — not shipped (ADR 0010 is Windows-only by current target list). The macOS MRT2 CI artifact is an opt-in QA build only; signing/notarization and a real model-inference test remain release requirements.

## Recent additions (last two weeks)

High-level summary of what landed on top of the 2026-09-14 release-readiness candidate. Each item maps to one or more git commits and lives in `src/` today.

### DAW migration

- **BRING A SONG transfer wizard** — Project Browser imports up to 32 aligned audio files and one Standard MIDI file into a new KYX project. Audio becomes named arrangement lanes with editable pre-import output track names; one optional reference mix can be marked muted. MIDI note tracks become editable song lanes, melodic note-off release velocity maps to per-note expression, CC7/10/11 map to track-volume/pan automation, CC64 sustain extends note lengths, and GM drums map to the kit grid. Song MIDI export writes supported track-gain/pan automation back as CC7/CC10; incoming CC7 and CC11 share one KYX gain curve, so their source distinction is not retained and export uses CC7. Gain above unity is capped on MIDI export because CC7 cannot represent it. The selected project tempo/meter and chosen source DAW are saved as versioned project metadata and shown in the project library. MIDI note/controller timing is converted through the source tempo map into the chosen constant project tempo, preserving elapsed timing against aligned WAVs; tempo-change markers are not retained as an editable map. Time-signature changes flatten to the selected meter. The dialog discloses conversions and losses before import, requires alignment confirmation, shows aggregate audio size, and warns when encoded audio exceeds 100 MB because decoded PCM can use considerably more memory. The empty-library first-run card separates KYX project-file import, DAW audio/MIDI transfer, Web Audio device support and plug-in hosting status before presenting its transfer CTA. The first time an imported project opens, a source-aware walkthrough explains alignment, the pattern/song distinction, supported MIDI conversion, action search, save and export; its completion is independent of the general onboarding tour. Levels/effects printed into WAVs remain in the rendered audio; source fader/pan controls are not recreated. Limits: 25 MB per audio file, 10 MB MIDI, 8 melodic tracks, 32-bar drum grid; no native FLP/CPR/PTX/AAF/OMF, other controller data, pitch bend, plug-in state, routing or source audio automation import. `npm run test:browser:daw-transfer` verifies a versioned 8-audio/4-MIDI import, IndexedDB reopen, media restoration, per-lane live MIDI playback and a non-silent offline render in Chromium. Real source-DAW export qualification remains open. See `docs/ROADMAP-DAW-MIGRATION.md` and `docs/DAW-TRANSFER-EXPORT-GUIDE.md`.

### Instruments

- **KOPA — kick designer (ADR 0035; KICK HARMONICS, ADR 0036)** — the 24th instrument: pitch envelope, CLICK transient, BODY, TAIL and phase-locked SUB, plus KICK HARMONICS: a body-phase-locked partial layer following the kick pitch sweep (2nd–16th partial, independent decay, CLEAN/SAT/FOLD), distinct from KOREŇ, which tracks incoming audio. Its six layers feed a 4-curve drive stage, `punch` glue and stereo spread. DSP lives in a typed core (`src/kopa/dsp/kopaProcessor.ts`, 16 voices, oldest-steal, RNG-free) behind its own lazy worklet module (`public/kopa-worklet.js`, budget 32 KB) with the event-queue offline contract (`prepareOfflineRender` seeds `processorOptions.events` — exports sound exactly like live); a context without the module gets a real native kick fallback with the KICK HARMONICS layer, never silence. 16 factory presets (techno punch → trap long → drill knock → phonk clipped → … → hardstyle reverse). Joins the `drums` preset-intent family (v1 only KOPA) so "load the trap long preset on the drums" resolves. _(feat `kopa` wave)_
- **KOPA fallback mirrors the worklet's LEVEL (2026-10-25)** — the native fallback was **3.3–12.5 LU louder** than the shipped worklet (mean 7.8 LU) across all 16 factory presets, and the loudness map records the WORKLET path — so the fallback was measuring a sound the product never exports. Two structural causes sat behind it: the SUB layer's decay target was scheduled **before** the attack ramp it follows (`setTargetAtTime(0, when, τ)` after `exponentialRampToValueTime(level, when + 4 ms)`), so the ramp cancelled it and the sub held its level for the whole note; and the fallback carried a per-voice 0.9 with **no** worklet-style 0.5 output factor (a net ×1.8). Fixed in `src/instruments/kopaNode.ts`: the voice gain is `velocity × level`, the output stage scales by 0.5, and the sub decay target starts at the end of the attack ramp (`when + 4 ms`) — the convention the rest of the registry already uses. Measured after the fix: **−2.7…+1.5 LU (mean 0.1 LU)**; the residue is the timbre of two different syntheses, not level. The loudness map does not move — `npm run test:browser:factory-presets` still passes 16/16. Locked by `tests/kopa/fallback.test.ts` (12 specs: no decay target may be planned before the attack ramp it follows, and the level contract is pinned per voice and at the output). _(feat `63b8f3d2`)_
- **Flute** (kind #15) — breath-noise + delayed vibrato + legato glide (slide-from with a `GLIDE` knob) + overblow harmonic + formant / drive body. 10 factory presets covering drill slide leads, memphis / liquid / jersey / pan / whistle / breath / reed / air timbres. Browser factory audio QA 252/252. _(feat a2992f2)_

### Effects

- **Master spectrum + EQ integration** — Mastering overlays the live master spectrum with the existing draggable EQ response. The user explicitly adds the master EQ; gain and bypass edits use undoable master-effect commands, while frequency and Q stay in the insert editor. Mixer analysis remains read-only. Visualizer color parity is still open; see `docs/MUSIC-MAKER-2026-BENCHMARK.md`.

- **TWIST v2.3 (ADR 0037 addendum 4)** adds per-device A/B curve snapshots (`deviceState: "twist-curves-v1"`) with a MORPH rack parameter. The panel captures both snapshots, draws them as reference ghosts, and blends the effective settings in the host so ordinary automation and intensity-sourced macros can perform the transition. Frequency controls morph geometrically, while enums and the `AUTO` sentinel switch at the midpoint. The state sanitizer bounds and numeric-validates stored parameters; curve updates apply live without rebuilding the worklet, and undo/redo re-seeds the runtime from document state.

- **TWIST (skrutka — phase dispersion)** — the 54th effect: a first-party, all-pass-only disperser in its own lazy worklet module (`public/skrutka-worklet.js`, `scripts/build-skrutka-worklet.mjs`, budget 16 KB, measured ~3 KB). DISPERSION is an 8-section 2nd-order all-pass cascade whose group-delay hump sits at FOCUS and grows with the knob (`r = 0.15 + 0.83·dispersion`, up to ~8–11 ms at typical focuses): a steady tone keeps its level while a broadband transient smears — the "throw / gulp" character EQ and saturation cannot produce. PUNCH is a 3-section short cascade on `0.93·|punch|` whose sign chooses the delayed band (positive delays the body below FOCUS → click leads, harder attack; negative delays the click band above FOCUS → transient sinks), with the center flip silent at zero because the radius is zero there; `punch = 0` skips the stage bit-exactly. SPREAD offsets the focus per channel (±12 %), so `spread = 0` is bit-exact mono. Parameters ride the M2 AudioParam pattern (no port messages; first block adopts written values, later changes glide 30 ms with the block-length-corrected coefficient), so live == offline automatically and `mix = 0` is bit-exact dry; the module has no RNG. `src/effects/skrutka-curve.ts` is the closed-form group-delay model shared by the device panel and the tests, so the drawn curve can never drift from the DSP. Honest 1:1 `degraded` bypass until the lazy module lands; 8 factory presets (`skrutka-808-throw`, `kick-snap`, `trap-gulp`, `sub-weight`, `snare-crack`, `hat-air`, `dark-stab`, `bus-glue`) + role landings for bass/drums/lead/chords. **v2 (ADR 0037 addendum)** adds the dynamic-phase layer: `dyn` (signed ATTACK/BODY envelope drive with the 15 ms radius glide that prevents parametric pumping), `keyMode` PUMP/DUCK on a second worklet input (the KOREŇ `setSidechainInput` contract; kick-thrown / kick-cleared phase, bit-exact without a key), `shape` GULP/CHIRP/DOUBLE/RESO section distributions, `punchHz` (0 = AUTO) + `punchMix` (parallel blend), and a tempo-synced FOCUS LFO (`lfoRate`/`lfoSync` host-side, `lfoShape` SINE/TRI/RAND seeded from the persisted factory seed, `lfoDepth`, `lfoPhase` anchored at transport start). The panel curve is now shape-aware and editable (drag = focus × dispersion, one undo frame), draws the driven-radius band and LFO sweep range, shows a live telemetry dot, and gains BAND SOLO through the new monitor-only `MasterChain.setMonitorBand` / `AudioEngine.setMonitorBand` (the lowpass mirror of PHONE CHECK's highpass on the same live-only branch). The v1 parameter set renders bit-identically (frozen FNV hash `e6cb495b83b6556c`); 8 new presets (Kick Duck Phase / Attack Smear / Tail Liquid / LFO Throw / Chirp Rise / Double Gulp / Reso Vowel / Punch Band); 120 s soak: zero subnormals, heap ≤ 6 MB. **v2.1 (ADR 0037 addendum 2)** adds FIELD (`field` ±1: the two chains process MID/SIDE with a continuous crossfade — `+1` is bit-transparent for mono content, `−1` leaves the side dry; SPREAD repurposed to split the component humps; `|field| ≤ 1e-4` is the bit-exact legacy path), draws M/S curves in the panel, and upgrades hardening to flagship parity: 300 s MOTION soak (full cascade + dyn + key + random LFO + FIELD: zero non-finite, zero subnormals, heap 1.0 MB) + 300 s STATIC drift (0.00091 dB ≤ 0.003 dB); 4 more presets (Side Shimmer / Center Gulp / Field Wide / Mono-Safe Throw) → 20 `skrutka-*` entries. **v2.2 (ADR 0037 addendum 3)** adds DYNAMIC PUNCH (`dynPunch` ±1: the same envelope scores drive the PUNCH radius — ATTACK deepens it on transients, BODY on sustain; own 15 ms glide, ceiling 0.97; the drive can open the stage from `punch = 0`, so "only accents get the tilt" is a real patch; `dynPunch = 0` bit-exact v2.1), the panel's cooler punch driven band + PUNCH-DYN chip, a telemetry `punch` field, model phase unwrapping for high-Q blends, and 4 more presets (Hard Knock / Ghost Tamer / Live Drummer / Tail Breath) → 24 `skrutka-*` entries. **v2.3 CURVE SNAPSHOTS (ADR 0037 addendum 4)** stores two full rack curves (A/B) on the effect's `deviceState` (kind `twist-curves-v1`, sanitized; STORE/CLEAR is one undoable command) and adds the real `morph` rack param (0..1) — the node blends host-side (`morphTwistParams`, shared with the panel): no slot = strict no-op, one slot = snapshot ↔ live knobs, both = A→B, missing ids fall back to base; `morph` rides `setParameterAt`, so automation lanes and `source:"intensity"` macros drive it (intro = center gulp → drop = sides shimmer). Panel: STORE A/B/CLEAR, morph slider, dotted A/B ghosts, `MORPH x% A→B` chip, chips report the effective state; curve-less DSP renders are untouched. **v2.4** ships the PORTAL×TWIST tandem chains (`phase-spectral-throw`, `808-space-ladder`, `ghost-pad`, `hyper-glitch`; trims measured, 13 chains total) and semantic MOVES in the targeted-effect parser: "make the 808 throw", "wider hats, mono safe", "smear the attack on the lead" resolve to TWIST with FOCUS landed per track role (kick 250 / 808 90 / chords 700 / lead 1200 for THROW; 3200/900/1600/2400 for WIDER and MONO-SAFE), MONO-SAFE winning over WIDER, no hijack of a named effect, and honest decline without a target. **v2.5 SCENE PACK** ships nine curated A/B pairs across genres (Trap/Drill/House/Techno/DnB/Amapiano/Jersey/Hyperpop/Ambient: intro voicing A → drop voicing B) applied by ONE undoable command that stores the snapshots, sets MORPH to the neutral midpoint and binds the param to scene intensity through a `TWIST` macro (`source:"intensity"`, amount 1 → intensity 0..1 swings morph 0..1); the binding is idempotent, the panel select shows CUSTOM for manual STOREs, and the chips report the effective state. _(feat `skrutka` wave, ADR 0037 + v2/v2.1/v2.2/v2.3/v2.4/v2.5 addenda)_
  _(feat `skrutka` wave, ADR 0037 + v2/v2.1/v2.2/v2.3/v2.4/v2.5 addenda)_
- **TWIST v2.9 — Smooth mode transitions** — the stored and visible enum choices stay discrete while the worklet glides through intermediate SHAPE layouts/radius, KEY OFF→PUMP→DUCK response, and LFO SINE→TRI→seeded RAND with its existing 30 ms block-corrected smoothing. Fractional SHAPE is mirrored by the analytic curve model; the 300 s soak exercises intermediate SHAPE / KEY / LFO states.
- **TWIST v2.10 — True LFO focus telemetry** — the processor sends the actual LFO-modulated focus, the wrapper validates it, and the live panel plots it directly. RAND no longer appears as the panel's old SINE approximation, and the point follows the worklet's smoothed LFO shape/depth.
- **TWIST v2.11 — Live response curve** — the worklet's 20 Hz observer frame carries its smoothed curve parameters plus actual long/punch radii. The panel plots and reads out that wet response, so SHAPE/FIELD/LFO and automated or scene-driven MORPH appear as heard; the dynamic range shows both PUMP/DYNAMIC expansion and KEY DUCK reduction. The AudioParam audio path is unchanged.
- **TWIST v2.12 — Output-aware response** — WET DELAY keeps the design curve; OUT DELAY includes the PUNCH blend, rack MIX and OUTPUT gain; OUT LEVEL shows the resulting dB response and cancellation dips. The complex model includes fractional FIELD rotation and the component dry blends. Deep cancellation nulls are omitted from phase delay because their phase is undefined; the level graph still shows them. Telemetry carries smoothed MIX/OUTPUT for automation-aware plots; the audio path is unchanged.
- **TWIST v2.13 — Stereo-linked dynamics detection** — DYNAMIC and DYN PUNCH now follow linked stereo energy, so anti-phase and SIDE-only signals still trigger the envelope while dual-mono retains its nominal detector level. A processor regression renders SIDE-only material through FIELD +1 and verifies that DYNAMIC changes the output.
- **TWIST v2.14 — Recursive-state input hardening** — non-finite program and KEY samples become silence before reaching envelope followers or all-pass state; finite samples are bounded at ±8 (+18 dBFS), retaining generous float headroom without feeding extreme corrupted peaks into recursive state. Processor and shipped-worklet checks compare against equivalent zeroed-or-clamped renders and verify bounded recovery.
- **TWIST v2.15 — Phase-independent KEY detection** — sidechain KEY uses linked stereo energy, so anti-phase/SIDE-only keys trigger PUMP and DUCK; dual-mono and mono-key levels retain their established response. Processor and shipped-worklet regressions compare anti-phase KEY against dual-mono KEY.
- **TWIST v2.16 — Detector visibility and contextual controls** — the panel shows live DYNAMIC activity and the effective KEY response, including an explicit no-source state. Conditional PUNCH, LFO, KEY and MORPH controls explain when their settings cannot affect the sound; KEY telemetry is bounded to its valid gain range. UI and wrapper regressions pin the readouts, hints and malformed-frame rejection.
- **TWIST shipped-worklet browser gate** — Chromium renders the generated `public/skrutka-worklet.js` with a SIDE-only transient, a corrupted peak and an anti-phase stereo KEY. The test checks that DYNAMIC changes the rendered audio, the peak stays below the ±8 input ceiling, stereo KEY matches dual-mono response, KEY affects the program and no processor error occurs (`npm run test:browser:twist`).
- **PORTAL input-path hardening** — NaN/Infinity and extreme peaks are sanitized once before both the latency-aligned dry ring and MID/SIDE STFT paths; finite peaks are bounded at ±8 (+18 dBFS). MIX 0 can no longer pass corrupted samples downstream, while wet renders recover without contaminating later frames; source and shipped-bundle regressions cover both extremes.
- **REZONANS input-path hardening** — non-finite samples become silence and finite peaks are bounded at ±8 (+18 dBFS) before analysis, dry-delay storage, or active harmony; the same rule applies when harmony is off or MIX is zero. Source and shipped-worklet regressions compare bypass and active renders against sanitized equivalents and check bounded wet output.
- **PORTAL device panel (v2 W1+W2)** — PORTAL was the last first-party lazy worklet effect with no device panel, and the one whose entire identity is a spectrum. `src/ui/PortalPanel.tsx` draws **two traces on one log-frequency axis**: IN (the spectrum as analysed) and OUT (the same bins after the mode ran). The gap between them IS the effect — the feedback tail climbing above the source in DELAY, the focus band swelling while the rest falls away in RESONATOR, the input line still moving while the output line stops dead in FREEZE. RESONATOR shades the FOCUS±WIDTH span; DELAY/FREEZE mark the shifted region SHIFT feeds back on. The traces come from `SpectralMeters.bandsIn/bandsOut`: 64 log-spaced magnitudes taken **inside the frame callback**, once before the mode and once after, from the MID path only, with band edges built once in `prepare()` and both buffers allocated at construction so the frame path stays allocation-free. The worklet posts them **without transfer** (structured clone copies on the receiving side), so the audio thread hands over 128 bytes without allocating or detaching; `portal-node` validates length and every value and drops the whole frame if either trace is unusable. Two calibration facts are now pinned by tests: the FFT is unnormalised (a 0.5 sine lands near 128 in its bin, so the trace is divided by `fftSize`), and Nyquist is a frequency while bin width is Hz-per-bin — conflating them collapsed 63 of 64 bands onto bin 1. `src/ui/portalSpectrum.ts` holds the band→frequency geometry so the panel and the processor cannot drift apart while both still look correct. The panel never re-derives a spectrum host-side (that would draw a clean hump at FOCUS on a resonator that never rang), and `mix = 0` reports **BYPASSED** rather than a flat line, because the rack bypasses the STFT there by design. Metering is panel-scoped: enabled on mount, released on unmount. `tests/spectral-panel-telemetry.test.ts` (11 specs) + `tests/portal-panel.test.tsx` (13 specs).
- **SCENE STRIP (Mixer + Arrangement)** — the shared scene-intensity performance surface for the TWIST/GROW scene packs, mounted under the mixer toolbar (`src/ui/Mixer.tsx`) and in the arrangement scenes sidebar (`src/ui/ArrangementPanel.tsx`): one row edits the scene's static intensity through `setSceneIntensity` (the arrangement INTENSITY lane owns the curve; a STATIC/CURVE chip reports which one the engine follows), and one row per TWIST/GROW device across the project shows its active A/B pair with a quick picker that applies the pack pair through the same one-undo commands as the device panels — so both effects play from one place, the track name selects the strip's track, and CUSTOM marks hand-stored slots. Collapsible (persisted); `tests/ui/SceneStrip.test.tsx` (5 specs) pins the intensity commit, the per-device pair apply, the SCENE chip, the CUSTOM fallback and the per-scene picker; +1 for the per-scene packs wave's `tests/scene-packs.test.ts` (8 specs: command + undo + validation, scene.packs sanitizer, scheduler seam points carrying the scene's packs, GROW endpoint law at points, armed-mapping guard, TWIST slot swap + morph = intensity, override exit restore, stop takeover). **Per-scene pairs** (v2.6): a scene PINS which pack pair each TWIST/GROW device plays — `Scene.packs` (fxId → pair id; SCHEMA_VERSION 23, shape-sanitized, unknown ids inert) set by one undoable `setScenePack` command from the strip's accent-bordered scene picker. The override is performance state: the scheduler attaches the active clip's scene packs to the scene-intensity points (live windows AND the offline `buildSceneIntensityPoints` — seam rule keeps the later point, so a pair swaps exactly on the boundary), and the bridge writes the resolved pair at each point — GROW `mid + (b−a)/2·bipolar` on every pair param, TWIST `setCurves(a, b, when)` + `morph = intensity` — while skipping armed mappings on those params (one writer), re-seeding the doc slots/values when the override leaves (scene without packs, gap, or stop). Intro/build/drop scenes therefore switch pairs automatically, identically in playback and exports. **Scene macro pins** (v2.7): a scene can also PIN macro values (`Scene.macros`, macroId → 0..1; SCHEMA_VERSION 24, clamped/sanitized, set by one undoable `setSceneMacro`) — while the scene is active the pinned value substitutes the live macro in every `source:"macro"` mapping (effect/instrument params and the track gain/pan macro offsets) through the same bipolar law, live and in exports (the scheduler attaches the scene `macros` to the same intensity points; a stop drops the pins and the doc values resume). The scene strip gains a MACROS block (compact faders showing the effective value; pinned = accent + × clear; moving a fader pins), and the mixer bar stays the live control. **Scene CC remap arming** (v2.8): a scene arms which `source:"midiCC"` mappings listen while it plays — `Scene.ccRemaps` (mapping-id allow-list; SCHEMA_VERSION 25; absent = all armed, `[]` = none, sanitized with the empty list preserved), set by one undoable `setSceneCcRemap` (canonicalizes a full list back to absent). The scheduler carries `ccRemaps` on the same intensity points, `MidiInput.handleCC` asks the engine (`sceneAllowsMidiMapping`) before sweeping a macro, a stop lifts the gate — live-only by nature (hardware CC never reaches an export), workspace `config.ccMappings` untouched. The strip gains a CC row (checkbox per mapping, armed bright / disarmed dim). **Scene CC re-points** (scene wave): a scene also re-points WHERE a mapping listens — `Scene.ccOverrides` (mappingId → { ccNumber 0..127, channel 1..16 }; SCHEMA_VERSION 29, sanitized), set by one undoable `setSceneCcOverride` that canonicalizes (a field equal to the mapping's own value drops, the empty record collapses to absent — typing the base value back is the clear gesture); `MidiInput.handleCC` resolves `engine.sceneCcOverride(id)` before matching, then the arming gate. The same wave consolidated the scene plumbing into one shape (`src/project-model/scene-performance.ts`: `ScenePerformance` + `scenePerformanceOf`; `SceneIntensityPoint extends ScenePerformance`, live call `setSceneIntensity(value, scene)`), so pattern, song-window and export points share one definition of the scene arc; the strip's CC row gains compact CC/CH number fields (NumericDraftInput, 0 = Any). **Scene modulator patterns**: a scene pins the modulation pattern too — `Scene.modulators` (lfoId → { steps?, seed? }; SCHEMA_VERSION 31, sanitized), set by one undoable `setSceneModulatorPattern` (bipolar-clamped steps, no canonicalization — a pin must survive later doc edits; × clears). The bridge patches the EFFECTIVE Lfo before each pure modulator call, so the step modulator plays the pinned steps and the random S&H draws from the pinned seed; live, `scheduleWindow` tracks the window's scene and passes `scenePerformanceOf(windowScene)` to the tail `applyModulators` sweep, and offline the renderer attaches each window's scene performance to `scheduleModulatorsOffline` — exports hear the same arc. The strip renders a row per schedulable LFO (STEP n / S&H n): PIN captures the current pattern (random without a seed gets a deterministic scene-scoped seed), the pinned row shows `STEPS ×n` / `SEED …` + clear ×. **In-strip step editor**: a pinned STEP row renders the shared `StepGridEditor` right in the strip (compact `height` prop; MOD keeps 64 px) — drag paints the scene-pinned steps with one commit per stroke, and the 8/16/32 tools resize through the same one-undo `setSceneModulatorPattern`; drawing no longer detours through MOD. The shared editor gained the ref-authority hardening (Slider audit-16 pattern): `paint` computes eagerly into `liveRef` and `commit` reads the ref, so a fast click that batches pointerdown+up in one flush still commits the painted value (pinned by a flick regression test). **Scene arc share codes** (PFARC1): one scene's whole performance context — pack pins, macro pins, CC arming scope + re-points, modulator patterns, intensity — travels as a single lz-string token (`src/export/sceneArcCode.ts`, same family as PFKIT1/PFBIND1/PFPACK1). Ids never travel: extraction keys entries by track/macro names, CC identity and modulator kind + track + target; `applySceneArc` (ONE undo) resolves them onto the target project — packs by track name with effect-kind fallback, macros/mappings by name + CC identity, modulators by kind + track + target — skips unmatched pins and counts them in the command detail (`N pins applied, M unmatched`). Dimensions the token carries REPLACE the scene's own; dimensions it lacks stay untouched; deviceState baselines are never modified. Codes are untrusted (clamped/rejected on decode); the strip's scene row gains COPY ARC (clipboard + transient status) and INSTALL ARC (paste dialog, honest `Invalid ARC code`). Locked by `tests/export/scene-arc-code.test.ts` (4 specs) + the strip suite (copy → install → error). _(scene-strip wave)_
- **KOREŇ (Bass Harmonic Processor)** — the 53rd effect: a general-purpose, tracked missing-fundamental exciter for arbitrary incoming bass. It complements the source-synchronous kick harmonics built into KOPA and runs in its own lazy worklet module (`public/bass-harmonic-worklet.js`, `scripts/build-bass-harmonic-worklet.mjs`, budget 24 KB). A control-rate YIN tracker on a ×4-decimated 300 Hz detection band finds the fundamental inside the `focusHz × [0.5, 2]` window (out-of-window frequencies are left untouched — no invented harmonics); accepted input zero-crossings phase-lock the generated 2nd/3rd-leaning stack, harmonic-deficit generation (Goertzel at f0/2f0/3f0, LP-compensated) avoids doubling partials the input already carries, and `character` shapes the generated layer CLEAN/SAT/FOLD (Drill Growl ships FOLD). Performance controls (wave 3): `sustain` compresses the generator envelope (`env^gamma`) so quiet bass passages stay readable, `retrig` restarts phase/envelope on note-ons (staccato lines glue to the attack instead of trailing like a pad), `width` rotates only the partials above 120 Hz for stereo with the sub kept mono, and `focusHz` automation sweeps the tracking window (drop performance FX) with zero extra DSP. Wave 4 adds routing and live UX: `keyMode` (OFF/PUMP/DUCK) gives the worklet a second input carrying a sidechain key from any track or bus through the engine's generic `setSidechainInput` contract — the key envelope (2 ms/180 ms) scales the GENERATED layer only, so PUMP blooms the harmonics on every kick and DUCK clears them out of its way; a throttled `type:"koren"` telemetry frame (observer-only, ~20 Hz) feeds the dedicated panel (live detected f0 + harmonic-stack bars over the focus window and the 120 Hz mono split) and the rack's "+x dB harmonics" mini meter; PHONE CHECK on the panel auditions the whole monitor path through a ~350 Hz highpass — live-only, offline renders branch it out of the graph by construction. Parameters ride the M2 AudioParam pattern (no parameter port messages — offline automation is native), so live and offline renders are identical; `mix = 0` is a bit-exact dry passthrough and the DSP is RNG-free. Four factory presets (808 Phone / Club Sub / Drill Growl / Subtle Body) with measured loudness gains; honest 1:1 `degraded` bypass until the module lands. Content surfaces (wave 5): the **BASS PHONE READY** chain (KOREŇ + air EQ) in the device **+ CHAIN** menu, the `phone` production-intent concept ("phone-ready bass", "na telefóne", "small speakers" → KOREŇ + air EQ; the telephone-lo-fi match is outranked in the same clause), and a Mix Doctor `harmonic-deficit` advisory (sub vs 120–350 Hz share, diagnosis-only, names KOREŇ as the repair). The intent-model GBNF grammar was re-pinned for the new concept (`intent-model:manifest` + `intent-model:validate`; the ONNX model itself is unchanged). Presence band (wave 6): the PHONE CHECK exposed an honest gap — the old stack's 2nd/3rd (110–220 Hz at f0=55) sit BELOW the 350 Hz a phone reproduces, so the effect was nearly inaudible through its own check; `presence` now redistributes the (16-partial) stack into 350–1200 Hz before normalization, nearly doubling the audible phone-band energy (×1.8 vs legacy, ×3.6 vs dry through a 350 Hz highpass) with the total level unchanged — 808 Phone ships `range 4` + presence 1. The stale-lock release gate yields to a fresh note-on (150 ms retrig hold), so the staccato glue survives the tracker's re-lock window while fades and unpitched gaps still release. Glide refinement (wave 7): the interval between accepted crossings refines f0 between YIN updates, so an exponential pitch sweep tracks within ~2 % (pre-refinement baseline ~5 %) while a plausibility clamp keeps harmonic-spiked intervals from yanking the root. Engine hint (wave 8): the engine writes the track's current MIDI note into the effect as `hintHz` native AudioParam automation (0 on note-off / panic) — a hint change snaps the root with zero acquisition latency (generation present within the first envelope attack instead of waiting ~90 ms for YIN), keeps generation legitimate while an untrackable or refilling window cannot lock, and refreshes the deficit at the known pitch; between changes YIN/refinement still fine-tune toward the audio, and clearing the hint hands acquisition back to pure YIN. Timbre profilers (wave 9): `phase` adds a Schroeder quadratic phase profile to the partials — same spectrum and RMS at nearly half the presence-band crest (11.4 → 5.6 dB, a smoother waveform that fits more level); `tilt` (±100 %) slopes the stack from a saw-like 1/n tail to a darker 1/n². Presets adopted (measured): 808 Phone phase 0.4, Drill Growl phase 0.3 + tilt 0.4, Subtle Body phase 0.6 + tilt −0.25; Club Sub deliberately stays the legacy aligned reference. 300 s flagship soak (wave 10): MOTION stress (FOLD + phase 1 + presence 1 + width 1 + key + hint over a staccato pattern with a glide) and STATIC drift (10 s vs 280 s ≤ 0.003 dB — measured 0.00025 dB) both hold zero non-finite/subnormal output AND zero subnormal internal one-pole state, heap ≤ 1.1 MB, flat CPU; the soak justified the denormal flushes added to `deficit2/3`/`envFast`/`envLag` (without them a rich input pulled `deficit2` to 4.9e-324 within 120 s). The lazy worklet list was renamed `PLUGIN_WORKLET_TYPES` → `LAZY_WORKLET_TYPES` (it now carries a first-party effect, not only vendored suites). Scene pack (wave 12): nine curated A/B pairs (`src/effects/koren-scene-pack.ts` — Drill Phone → Growl, Trap 808 → Weight, House Pluck → Liquid, Techno Rolling → Drone, DnB Sub → Reese, Amapiano Log → Breath, Jersey Tick → Club, Hyperpop Clean → Fold, Ambient Still → Bloom) applied by ONE undoable command: the slots land in deviceState (`koren-scene-v1`, sanitized + clamped), every differing param takes the midpoint as its live base plus an intensity-sourced mapping with `amount = (b − a)/(max − min)`, so the engine's own symmetric offset law resolves scene intensity 0 exactly to side A (the phone intro) and 1 to side B (the drop) — GROW needs no DSP-side morph scalar; stepped ids (CHARACTER/RANGE/RETRIG) switch at their rounding boundary mid-swing, the command owns and rewrites the effect's intensity binding (never stacks; macro-sourced mappings untouched), and the panel select falls back to CUSTOM for hand-stored slots. _(feat `bassHarmonic` wave, ADR 0034 + wave-2..wave-12 addenda)_
- **Morph Dynamics** — fifth flagship plugin. First-party DSP, dedicated `morph-dynamics-worklet.js`, panel under `src/ui/MorphDynamicsPanel.tsx`. _(feat `no dobre teraz` / `okay vša`)_.
- **Granular Freeze send** — texture engine wrapped as a send-effect: freeze latch captures a window, granular cloud (64 grains, 4× overlap, Hann) plays the frozen buffer, dry path is ducked by the same envelope so the send stays usable (not a stack). Released crossfade returns to the live signal. 5 factory presets (Pad Hold / Shimmer Cloud / Deep Drone / Glitch Cloud / Tape Hold). _(feat `700c20d`)_
- **Telephone preset pack** — SVF narrow-band + distortion character for the lo-fi phone / handset chain beatmakers reach for on hooks and fills. 5 pure-preset entries (svf-telephone-band / svf-telephone-band2 / svf-radio-mid / dist-telephone / dist-intercom); no new DSP. _(feat `700c20d`)_
