- **Beatmaking expansion** — tapeStop, ringMod, freqShifter, pitchShift, vinyl, beatMangler, multiTapDelay + bassMono master utility. Each ships a dedicated worklet processor, panel UI, factory presets and regression / golden tests. _(feat `0899762`)_
- **Vocoder + Reverse Swell** — two new character send-effects for the FX rack. _(feat `b3a6c69` family)_
- **An OFFLINE render starts an effect from the DOCUMENT's parameter values** — the construction-time smoothing (20 ms time constant) is a LIVE affordance, not an export rule: the realtime chain is built before the transport plays, so the ramp has long settled by the time a note is audible, while the OFFLINE chain is built AT the render origin and the very same ramp is still in flight when the first event lands (50 ms = 2.5 time constants → the parameter sits at 91.8 % of the way to its target). The export therefore started from the NODE's default values — saturation's tone at the biquad default of 350 Hz against the document's 8000 Hz, multiband's solo gate gliding from 1 to 0 — and the live↔offline null measured −17…−33 dB against a −40 dB floor while the offline↔offline control stayed at ≈ −330 dB. In the offline regime the construction write lands INSTANTLY (`setValueAtTime`), every registry write now goes through ONE helper (`smooth` — 27 direct `setTargetAtTime` calls converted), and a write scheduled LATER than the construction moment keeps its glide (a ramp the live engine would have heard is a ramp the export has to hear). Automation is untouched — it writes through the automation bridge, never through `smooth()`. Measured after the fix: multiband −24.3 → **−67.0 dB**, saturation −33.4 → **−67.0 dB**, clipper −29.4 → **−72.2 dB**, distortion **−67.9 dB**, drumBuss **−75.5 dB**, bassBuss **−70.0 dB**. Two worklet effects stay out of the null corpus with a measured reason (a free-running modulation phase, the same category as phaser/flanger/chorus/tremolo/autowah): kaskada's echo timing follows a per-sample LFO (the head of the window matches at −66.4 dB, the tail diverges at −9.1 dB) and wow/flutter modulates its delay the same way (tail null +0.5 dB) — both with an exact offline determinism control (−316.7 / −325.2 dB). Guard: `tests/offline-effect-params.test.ts` (8 specs: instant vs glide, the mix bus opens instantly offline, a scheduled write keeps its glide, the regime is scoped to the factory call and restored on a throw, and the engine routes the regime by CONTEXT TYPE).
- **The live↔offline null gate reports HOW MANY cases failed, not how many fit in one line** — the failure message listed the FIRST EIGHT failures and nothing else, so a gate that had been red for sixteen cases reported eight of them; fixing those eight then REVEALED the next eight instead of turning the gate green (the 2026-10-25 run showed a completely different set of eight cases than the baseline, for the same gate). The verdict was always honest (`ok = failures.length === 0`); the MESSAGE was not — it hid the size of the hole. It now reads `failed=N/total (first 8 shown)`: **4 of 61** cases fail — the four FLAGSHIP effects whose live branch holds state the offline branch cannot reproduce (ozvena −39.2 dB, morphdynamics −29.7 dB, bassHarmonic −27.2 dB, portal −7.1 dB; every one with an exact offline determinism control, and every one a LOCAL divergence — the magnitude spectra agree to 0.9999–1.0000 while the waveform differs in the head or in the tail, so these are not phase cases). The three free-running-phase effects that failed beside them (ringMod, pitchShift, vinyl) are now EXCLUDED with a measured reason — the same class as phaser/flanger/chorus/tremolo/autowah — which is what took the corpus from 64 cases to 61. The choral instrument case, which rendered **silence** in the parity case (rms 0.000×) while its own note-on check rendered a signal (peak 0.759) — a VACUOUS case: CHORÁL is a "drop-a-hum" instrument whose worklet sustains an UPLOADED source, so with no source both branches render silence and the case "fails" on a 0.0 dB null of two silent buffers. The corpus now hands the track a factory source, so the case measures a real −87.6 dB null at corr 1.0000 and the gate is GREEN on it. The remaining four each need their own root cause; all of them are pre-existing and were HIDDEN by the cap — the offline parameter regime did not cause them. The full taxonomy, measured per case, follows.
- **The seven measured live↔offline parity failures are WORKLET-STATE divergences, and the mechanism is now measured — three of them are now excluded with that measurement, four still need their own DSP analysis** — every case was measured individually with the probe (`scripts/verify-parity-probe.mjs`, per-case null, correlation, head/tail segment nulls and both peaks) and again by the gate itself on 2026-10-23 (`failed=7/64` — the choral case now PASSES, so these seven WERE the whole remaining red; after the three exclusions below the gate reports **4 of 61**). In EVERY case the LIVE branch is reproducible (live↔live null −240 … −324 dB) and the OFFLINE branch is reproducible (control −308 … −325 dB) — so this is not device-clock noise: the two branches genuinely compute different results, and each effect needs its own root cause. Two runs together add one more observation that a single run cannot make: **the null VALUE moves between runs (ringMod −0.8 dB in the probe run, +0.7 dB in the gate run) while the magnitude spectrum and the level agreement do not (0.9886–1.0000 cosine, 0.901–1.005× RMS)** — the branches produce the SAME SOUND from a different state, which is what separates these cases from a genuinely different render. The taxonomy: **ringMod** — null −0.8 dB, corr 0.586, best offset −193 samples, peaks 0.633/0.611, but the magnitude spectrum agrees (0.9998) and the level agrees (1.005×): the same sound, differently phased. The mechanism is in the code — the modulator phase free-runs (`this.phase += phaseInc` per sample, from the PROCESSOR's construction, `src/audio-worklets/ringmod-processor.js`), so the live branch (the chain has been running since the project opened) and the export branch (the chain is built at the render origin) modulate with a different phase. The fix is to anchor the modulator to the **transport timeline** (the precedent: the TEXTURE synth's "LFO clock anchored to the note timeline"), because the contexts' absolute frame counts already differ — nothing else is shared between the two branches. **pitchShift** — null +1.2 dB, corr 0.219, peaks 0.478/0.644 (−3.6 dB): phase AND level. **vinyl** — null −3.3 dB, corr 0.766, the difference sits in the TAIL (head −17.8 dB), peak +1.2 dB. **portal** — null −3.5 dB, corr 0.780, the difference sits in the TAIL (head −74.0 dB), peaks match. **ozvena** — null −40.0 dB (exactly on the floor), corr 1.0000, the difference sits in the TAIL (head −63.5 dB), peaks match: a tail-LEVEL difference only. **morphdynamics** — null −26.6 dB, corr 0.9998, the difference sits in the HEAD (−25.7 dB; tail −42.8), peak +0.5 dB: the ATTACK differs. **bassHarmonic** — null −33.2 dB, corr 0.9998, the difference sits in the TAIL (−24.9 dB; head −65.2), peaks match. The three LFO-family cases (ringMod, pitchShift, vinyl) are the SAME class as the five effects the corpus ALREADY excludes with a measured reason (phaser/flanger/chorus/tremolo/autowah — "free-running LFO phase"), so they now carry that exclusion too (with the measurement and the code site in the reason string); the code says why: the carrier's phase is seeded by the PROCESSOR's construction, not by the audio (ringmod-processor.js — `this.phase += phaseInc` from the constructor; pitchshift-processor.js — the tap delay accumulates `+= dRate` per sample from the constructor; vinyl-processor.js — `lfoPhase`/`flutterPhase` start at 0 and free-run). The live branch builds the chain right before the capture, the offline branch builds it at the render origin, so the two modulate from a different phase — and the gap between construction and the event is load-dependent, which is exactly why the null value moves between runs. The other four are NOT phase cases: their magnitude spectra agree to 0.9999–1.0000 while the WAVEFORM diverges LOCALLY (ozvena, bassHarmonic and portal in the TAIL, morphdynamics in the HEAD), so each of them needs its own DSP analysis — and that analysis is now done: **ozvena** (`engines/hallEngine.ts`: `lfoPhase` free-runs and modulates the FDN read positions — `modPhase = lfoPhase + (l / FDN_LINES) * Math.PI * 2` → `modOffset` — the same read-position modulator the excluded `reverb` carries, here in the flagship hall and plate engines), **morphdynamics** (`dsp/motion.ts`: `this.phase += (rateHz * this.blockSize) / this.sampleRate` — a per-block LFO accumulator), **bassHarmonic** (the detector keeps a PLL phase PER PARTIAL — `p = this.phase[n] + (twoPi * n * this.f0) / sr`, `this.phase[n] = p` — so the partials' phase at the second hit depends on how long the live instance has been running), **portal** (`spectral-core/dsp/SpectralProcessor.ts`: `this.motionPhase += (hz * frames) / this.sampleRate`, plus the resonator's per-bin phase history). All four are therefore the SAME root cause as the excluded family — per-instance state seeded by the PROCESSOR's construction instead of by the transport timeline — but their consequence is AUDIBLE (morphdynamics' attack −25.7 dB and +0.5 dB peak, portal's tail +1.9 dB, bassHarmonic's tail −24.9 dB, ozvena's tail −34.3 dB), so they are NOT excluded: for them the null is still a meaningful metric, and excluding them would hide a deviation the user can hear behind a green gate. The honest closure is the ARCHITECTURAL one — anchor the effect state to the transport timeline (the precedent is the TEXTURE synth's "LFO clock anchored to the note timeline"), which is an ADR-sized change to the engine and the worklets, not a one-line fix. Note also that the parity harness builds the live chain right before the capture, so it measures the SMALLEST possible version of this divergence: in a real session the chain has run for minutes, and the export's modulator phase then differs from the live one by an arbitrary amount that changes every time the project is played differently. The eighth case was not a divergence at all: **instrument:choral** was VACUOUS — CHORÁL is a "drop-a-hum" instrument whose worklet sustains an UPLOADED source, so with no source both branches rendered silence (windows=0) and the case "failed" on a 0.0 dB null of two silent buffers; the corpus now hands the track a factory source, and the case measures a real −87.6 dB null at corr 1.0000.
- **The dev server must not DISCOVER a dependency while the app boots** — Vite's dep-optimizer scans the entry graph, and a WORKER module is not in it (Vite bundles workers separately), so `@huggingface/transformers` was discovered only when the studio created the AI worker. A runtime discovery re-runs the optimizer, which changes the `?v=` hash of EVERY optimized dep: the page then held **two copies of React** (measured: `react.js?v=5e019cbd` AND `react.js?v=e2debdb6` → "Invalid hook call" + "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'useState')"), and Vite sent a **full page reload** that destroyed the DOM the app-boot gate was waiting for — reported as "UI did not mount". The dependencies a worker imports are now declared for the optimizer up front (`optimizeDeps.include`: `@huggingface/transformers`, `onnxruntime-web/wasm`), so the first pass already holds them and no re-optimize can happen mid-boot; the sibling's `zod` is **excluded** rather than included, because `include` resolves from the root (zod 4.5.4) while the sibling's source runs on zod 3.25.76 — the copy the production build uses. Measured after the fix: ONE React (`react.js?v=9de4bd4e`), no second optimizer pass, the studio mounts. Both configs also declare `resolve.dedupe: ["react", "react-dom"]` themselves: the shared QMR HUD source lives in the sibling checkout and resolves React 19.3.0 there against the app's 18.3.1 (measured). `@vitejs/plugin-react` contributes the same list — but the RESOLVED config cannot be asserted on (mergeConfig concatenates arrays, so a resolved check stays green even after the repo's own declaration is deleted), which is why the guard reads the files. Guard: `tests/vite-late-dep-discovery.test.ts` (5 specs: every worker import is declared for the optimizer, zod is excluded and NOT included, the scan is pinned to the app entry, both configs declare the dedupe, the sibling's different React is measured), proven able to go red by removing either declaration.
- **A refused OPTIONAL local service is not a boot failure — but only when every failed request went to it** — the gallery/collab server (`server/collab-server.mjs`) is an optional process: without it the studio is local-only, and the IntakeTray polls its `/api/intake` endpoint every 8 s while the BROWSER logs each refusal as a console error (the app itself swallows the failure and stays quiet, and it cannot suppress a network-layer log). Measured 2026-10-22: six refusals to `http://127.0.0.1:1234/api/intake` turned the app-boot gate red even though the studio booted fine. The gate now records the **failed requests with their URLs** — the console text carries none — and ignores a refusal only when every failed request went to that optional service, so a real network fault still turns it red. The gate is the browser check itself (`scripts/verify-browser.mjs`), so it has no vitest guard; the invariant register therefore does not carry a row for it (every register row must name a guard that contains `expect(`), and the fact lives here instead. The same run reports the ignored refusals explicitly: `[info] app boot — ignored 6 refused request(s) to the optional local gallery service`.

### Sample bank expansion

- Kick bank 6 → 15 — drill / phonk / 808s / vintage / club punches / boom-bap knock; genre kits carry dedicated kicks.
- Snare bank 4 → 9 — drill crack, phonk / jersey / dnb backbeats, lofi dust.
- Hat bank 5 → 10 — drill tick, phonk dusty, jersey / dnb metallic pings, open cup; hat.pedal synth pulled apart from closed.soft (0.993 duplicate pair). _(feat `38b8fd0`, `378ab36`)_
- Pop wave — kick.pop, clap.pop stack, crash.pop, tom.floor, rim.pop, shaker.pop + kalimba/musicbox mallets (86 → 94 assets, all with curated WAVs; orphan backfill: cello/nylon/orchestrahit/pizzicato/violin seeds).
- **Bank-wide kick pitch anchoring + hat.open.cup re-voice (library-gate wave)** — the 2026-09-29 semitone snap covered only the 808 family; the remaining 12 kick glide rests now land on exact semitones too (F1 soft/techno, F#1 deep, G1 trap/phonk/lofi, G#1 dnb/909/pop, A1 punch/jersey/knock; knock's mid knocker layer rests on D3) — verified by rest-window measurement within ±24 ¢. cup re-voiced into its "Open, Darker" identity for real (3800 Hz dark ring + 0.5 s wash + loud 4300 Hz ping — a first 4600 Hz nudge measured d=0.066, i.e. WORSE: half-measures read as twins; distance to hat.open now 0.18, to hat.wash 0.11 with a distinct mechanism — tonal ping vs pure air sweep).
- **clap.pop re-voice (library-gate wave)** — the shipped seed stacked clap.main + clap.soft verbatim; after mastering pulled all claps to −10.5 the stack collapsed onto main (feature distance 0.051 — the tightest pair left). Re-voiced as the gated bright pop clap: 2000 Hz register (vs main/soft ~1100), 80 ms gated tail, snap layer (centroid 2108 → 2831, decay 110 → 82 ms, distance 0.19/0.29). Lesson kept: tap spacing alone measured sub-perceptual (first pass d=0.046) — clap separation lives in register + decay.
- **Per-band attack term + de-homog wave 2 (2026-10-04)** — the audit's redundancy vector could not see a strike: a whole-file band share averages a sub-ms mallet contact into a rounding error, so the 2026-10 strike fixes measured **0.0-0.1 dB broadband** and every strike change was invisible. New `src/analysis/transientProfile.ts` (shared by the audit script and `tests/transient-profile.test.ts`, so the printed table, the feature vector and the lock can never drift) measures the leading-window energy **per band** and the feature vector's `attack` term is now that per-band max. Two correctness guards, both earned by real numbers: a **participation floor** (a band whose loud-span power sits >30 dB under the loudest band's has no body to measure a transient against — it would win the max on filter ringing divided by near-zero) and **zero-phase forward-backward filtering** (a causal IIR reads its own settling transient into the head window — a steady tone scored a fake 2.4 dB on an empty band). Result on the real pre/post mallet pairs: **broadband 0.0-0.1 dB vs per-band 1.5-3.8 dB**. Six re-voices then moved six watchlist pairs off the list: `clap.main↔clap.soft` 0.121 (main's tail band 1100 → 1240 Hz, decay 0.16 → 0.11), `clap.main↔clap.trad` 0.131 → 0.183, `hat.pedal↔hat.phonk` 0.081 (pedal commits to the dry foot-splash: 4000 Hz / 25 ms; phonk gains a metallic 4800 Hz ping over a 3400 Hz wash — a plain dark hat left them at 0.071 as featureless noise), `kick.pop↔kick.punch` 0.096 (punch commits down: decay 0.34 / click 0.4, body-as-voice instead of another bright pop), `kick.909↔kick.trap` 0.073 (909's heritage identity: own overdrive 0.28 + 0.38 s ring), `snare.dnb↔snare.tight` 0.101 (the crack band now reads four distinct pockets — main 1750 room backbeat / tight 2200 thin-high ghost at B3 / dnb 2500 genre / jersey 2800 club crack). Remaining 7 watchlist pairs are the banded-dual-criterion "family" band by design (`kick.knock↔kick.lofi` 0.066 and `hat.closed.soft↔hat.closed` 0.091 are the documented locked compromises; `snare.drill` sits 0.110-0.130 from three of its genre siblings by being a genre-locked crack). _(feat `99ec9550`, `e266c48d`)_
- **Mallet trio mastered + sound-library gate (library-gate wave)** — the 3 synth-only slots (vibes/marimba/celesta, which sat ~10 dB above the mastered kit) joined the curated layer (95 → 98 WAVs, every factory asset now curated); new `tests/sound-library-gate.test.ts` locks the audit permanently — source-greps the category targets from the seed renderer and asserts every shipped WAV's loudness/DC/grid-alignment/format (would have caught all six defect classes the 2026-09 audit repaired).
- **Bass + Lead expand (Priority-1 wave, 2026-10-08)** — 13 new curated assets (98 → 111 WAVs): the bass family 6 → 14 (melodic 808 as SOFT/MEDIUM/HARD velocity zones, held sub sine + sub square, upright jazz walking bass, acid 303 squelch at two rates) and a NEW **Lead category** (5 assets: workhorse saw, 7-osc supersaw anthem, chiptune square, bright/dark filter plucks). Every voice feeds the UN-SUNO bass/lead lanes and re-style directly. Seed renderer gained the Lead category treatment (−14 LUFS, glue drive 0.12); the seed script now pins `optimizeDeps.entries` (untracked sibling HTML artifacts poisoned the dep-scan). Locked by the expanded (9/9: registry coherence for both packs, per-voice tuning anchors F1/E1/C2/D2, 808 zone escalation) + the standing sound-library gate (4/4).
- **Cymbal/shaker expand (Priority-2 wave, 2026-10-09)** — 4 new curated assets (111 → 115 WAVs) for the jazz/dnb/rock families: the **china cymbal** (the only crash-family voice with a trash/roar character — two beating tritone partials, explosive short tail), the **jazz ride wash** (the ping's complement: a tick-led long wash for swing comping), the **fast shaker** (the third shaker voice: a double-hit 16th accent, not a swish), and the **crash roll** (an accelerating crescendo into a bloom — the transition/build fill). Locked by (6/6: registry coherence, china shorter than main wash, jazz ride outlives the ping, double-hit RMS, crescendo peak-vs-opening ≥3×).
- **World percussion (Priority-3 wave, 2026-10-09)** — 6 new curated assets (115 → 121 WAVs) for amapiano/latino/afro/samba: the conga **quinto** (slap tone — the existing conga keeps the open tumbao; brighter by ZCR, locked), **bongos** (the martillo pair as one asset: hembra + macho 30 ms apart — two transients locked), **timbale** (metal-shell cascara crack), **tabla** (the syahi pitch GLIDE 180→145 Hz — the bend IS the instrument; sustains longer than conga, locked), **cajón** (box thump + snare-buzz tapa — the lowest-pitched percussion voice, locked), and **agogô** (the samba double-bell chase). Locked by `tests/world-perc-pack.test.ts` (6/6). Percussion family: 13 → 19.
- **Field / found-sound pack (2026-10-12)** - the one lane the 142-sample library had no home for: the sound of a **place** rather than an instrument, which current production treats as a first-class voice (room tone under a lo-fi bed, a switch click as a transient, gravel as percussion). **9 synthesized voices in a new `Field` category** (14 categories now, `factory.field.*`): four **beds** meant to sit under a beat — `roomtone` (pink-noise indoor air), `fridge` (100 Hz motor + mains-locked harmonics with a slow thermostat cycle), `mains` (50/100/150 Hz + a 2.4 kHz ballast buzz), `street` (brown-noise traffic wash with one pass-by at 2.3 s) — and five **hits** — `gravel` (three boots, dense granular bursts), `paper` (turn swell + front-loaded crease grains), `rustle` (amplitude-modulated fabric friction), `switch` (2 ms snap + 1.8 kHz contact ring), `match` (scrape into a flame bloom). Design rules the whole pack follows: seeded via `mulberry32` so the render is byte-identical every boot (the determinism invariant covers factory content), written straight into a buffer rather than an oscillator stack, level far below the drum targets because foley should be felt not fought, and the four beds are **flat and stationary** (no slow LFO sweeps) so they loop without a seam — the same contract `fxVinyl` already holds. `Field` gets its own seed-renderer treatment (`targetLufs: -22`, `tapeDrive: 0.02` — "tape" is the opposite of what a field recording should sound like, the hiss must stay in the recording) and its own kit colour. Locked by `tests/field-recordings-pack.test.ts` (7: manifest parity, builder+duration, the bed/hit length split, determinism seeds, category plumbing, and that no curated WAV appears — the synthesized path is the source of truth here); **audibility is proved in the browser** by `npm run test:browser:factory-presets`, because the vitest setup has no Web Audio polyfill and cannot render buffers. Chosen from an internet survey of 2026 production trends, after mapping every trend signal against the existing DSP inventory first — `tapeSat`/`vinyl`/`bitcrusher`/`granularFreeze`/`vocalchop` already cover the lo-fi and granular lanes, so the honest gap was **content, not effects**.
- **Wave-17 follow-ups (2026-10-13)** — four items closed off the dusty-jazz and wow-&-flutter waves. **(a) `jazz-kit` actually carries the brushes its description promises.** It shipped reading "brushed backbeat" while containing no brush; nothing failed, because a description is a free-text string and no test compared it to the pads. `factory.brush.tip` takes the PEDAL hi-hat slot (the closed hat carries `HAT_DYNAMIC` and is the kit's time-keeping pulse; buying a brush by deleting it would be a bad trade) and `factory.brush.swirl` takes the BELL slot, not the ride slot, because pad 10 is the washey ride the description names first — a 1.1 s attackless sweep over that wash IS the jazz brushed backbeat (the ding stroke). Neither brush takes a choke group, mirroring `dusty-jazz-kit`: the swirl has to bloom OVER the ride, and sharing `chokeGroup 2` would let the sweep silence the comping it swells. `ride.bell` keeps its home in `dusty-jazz-kit`. The guard for the CLASS is `every kit description is backed by the assets that kit selects` in `tests/dusty-jazz-family.test.ts`: every instrument word in a kit description must be backed by an asset the kit selects, with diacritic folding so the latin kit's "cajón"/"agogô" resolve to `cajon`/`agogo`. Verified falsifiable — reverting the two pads turns it red and names the kit, the word and the asset list it actually has. **(b) The drift gate covers the sample library.** `collectCountChecks` tracked only Effects / Instruments / Project templates / spec files, so the dusty-jazz wave could move assets 151→155 and categories 14→15 with nothing noticing. It now checks Factory assets, Asset categories and Genre kits as well, derived from the **imported arrays** rather than parsed out of source text — a regex over the manifest silently drops ids containing characters outside `[a-z0-9.]` (`factory.fx.riser-short`), which is how a count reaches "close enough" instead of "right". "Asset categories" counts distinct categories **actually in use**, deliberately not union members, so an unused union member surfaces as a gap instead of inflating the number. `tests/drift-check.test.ts` pins the label list, and it went red on the first run of the change exactly as intended. **(c) `runChecks` loads the real-instrument packs before the preset audit.** `src/browser-checks.ts` measured the piano and VSCO presets against their silent synth fallback because `ensurePianoPackLoaded` / `ensureVscoPackLoaded` were missing there while `scripts/verify-factory-presets.mjs` already had them; the two awaits now sit between `loadCuratedLayer` and `auditFactoryPresetAudio`, mirroring the script. **(d) Wow &amp; flutter's knob readout is honest.** The depth knob asked for cents it could not deliver: below ~0.5 Hz the requested swing exceeds the ring's whole budget, so the number next to the knob was a claim, not a measurement. The modulation math moved out of the processor into `src/audio-worklets/wowflutter-math.js` — a worklet-free module, because importing the processor from the UI bundle throws (the class extends `AudioWorkletProcessor`), which is exactly why the readout could not be written before — and `wfSwingPlan` is now the processor's **own allocation**, extracted rather than reimplemented, so the panel and the DSP cannot disagree. `ParamDef` gained an optional `secondary(values)` and the grid renders it as a second line under the knob, visible only when the two disagree; it shows nothing at all on a correct setting. Measured: **60 cents requested at 0.3 Hz deliver 32.3 cents**, and the panel now says so. Locked by 7 new specs in `tests/wow-flutter.test.ts`, including one that measures the rendered delay trajectory and compares it against the plan — falsified by scaling the processor's amplitude 1.5× alone, which turns it red with `measured 46.75 ct vs planned 32.27 ct`.
- **Dusty jazz / boom-bap family (2026-10-13)** — 4 synthesized voices (151 → 155) in a new **`Brush`** category (15th, kit colour `#c4b5fd`), each expressing a physical mechanism rather than adding more of the same: `factory.brush.tip` (the stroke — broadband noise under a ~54 Hz tuft-bounce rattle, no drum body, 0.3 s), `factory.brush.swirl` (the continuous sweep — 1.1 s with **no attack transient at all**, because bristles never leave the head; bandpass 2400 → 5200 Hz, rattle 38 → 72 Hz), `factory.snare.ghost` (snare **wires with no shell** — two detuned high partials for wire buzz, 0.12 s), and `factory.ride.ding` (a dark stick-led ride: inharmonic partials 1 / 1.41 / 1.68 / 1.98 / 2.34 at ~2.6 kHz under a **lowpass** 5.2 kHz wash, an inversion of the highpassed 5.4–7 kHz bright rides that were already the bank's only ride family). The genre's own physics is the spec: bristle rattle = fast AM, swirl = no attack, ghost = wires not shell, dark ride = lowpass where all others highpass. The vocabulary was mapped against the bank **before** building and deliberately not rebuilt: Rhodes/Wurlitzer, upright bass, swung hats, shaker, tambourine, ride wash and all tape/vinyl/12-bit grit already exist (the grit is DSP — `fxTapeSat`/`fxVinyl`/`fxBitcrusher`), and **vibraphone was already covered** by `factory.mallet.vibes` with its rotating-disc tremolo — nearly added twice. Seed treatment for `Brush` (`targetLufs: -19`, `tapeDrive: 0.1`, `tapeTone: 9500`, `ceiling: -2.2`) deliberately **inverts** the near-zero drive of `Field`: this genre is defined by 12-bit samplers and tape hiss, so a dead-clean brush reads as a digital hi-hat. Consuming kit **`dusty-jazz-kit`** ("Dusty Jazz / Boom-Bap", 16 pads) is the **12th** `KIT_PRESETS` entry and takes genre **`boombap`, not `jazz`** — measured before it existed: `boombap` was already a first-class genre in `GENRES` (`src/ai/types.ts`) that **no kit claimed**, so `getKitPresetsForGenre` fell through to its all-kits fallback and the dice could drop a boom-bap track on `trap-808` / `techno-drive` / `bright-perc` (measured pool: 12 of 12); `jazz` keeps `['jazz-kit']` so its pool test passes untouched. New `BOOM_BAP_SNARE_DYNAMIC` reuses the existing `dyn(ghost, mains, accent)` machinery, which had always modelled "soft hits are a different timbre" but had no real ghost to model it with — every stock set borrowed an existing tight snare as its ghost. `factory.snare.lofi` joins `RR_VARIATIONS` with a **wider-than-stock** spread (±1.9 % rate, 1.05/0.93 gain) because the lo-fi voice's character _is_ its imperfection, and a swung groove is exactly where identical backbeats read worst. Locked by `tests/dusty-jazz-family.test.ts` (12) with the owned suites green at 44/44 across 6 files (family, kick-bank, kit-voice-expand, kit-velocity-humanize, field-recordings-pack, sound-library-gate); audibility proved in the browser — all **643/643** assets including the 4 new voices render and pass the finite/unclipped audition (`npm run run presets:loudness` remains the sole failure, a pre-existing KOPA loudness-map drift outside this domain).
- **Kit + voice expand (2026-10-09)** — closes the "asset exists, nothing selects it" gap of the three expand waves: **3 new genre kits** in `KIT_PRESETS` (8 → 11) — Latin Percussion (`latin`: quinto/bongos/timbale/cajón/agogô/fast shaker), Jazz Combo (`jazz`: the washey ride as THE comping voice, soft kick, crash-roll bloom) and Rock Backline (`rock`: china accent, cracking snare, full toms, crash roll) — so the dice kit pool can finally draw every new percussion voice, and **13 sampler presets** give every new melodic bank voice a preset consumer (808 SOFT/MEDIUM/HARD → trap/trap/drill, sub sine → house, sub square → dnb, upright → score, acid fast/slow → techno; saw/supersaw/pluck-bright → house, square → jersey, pluck-dark → score) with roots pinned to the samples' recorded fundamentals (D1/F1/E1/C2/A3/A4). Locked by `tests/kit-voice-expand.test.ts` (8/8).
- **Velocity-dynamics presets (2026-10-09)** — the first `FACTORY_PRESETS` that carry `velocityLayers` (the multi-sample surface was pack-only — Salamander/VSCO — while the eager factory bank shipped 600+ single-sample presets): **4 dynamics presets** layering zone voices of the SAME recorded root with disjoint 0..1 windows, so played velocity changes TIMBRE, never pitch — 808 Dynamics (soft/medium/hard zones, trap), Sub Dynamics (sine → square growl, dnb), Acid Dynamics (held line → squelch accent, techno), Pluck Dynamics (dark ghost → bright accent, house). Sets live in `sample-library/velocity-layers.ts` next to the drum-side dynamics; `FACTORY_KICK_LAYERS` gained a documented pitch hazard (the semitone snap gave its four zones different tunings — kept for manual mapping flows, never preset-carried). **Bug fixed en route:** `applyInstrumentPreset`'s conditional spread kept stale layers on UNDO — a layered apply could never be undone. Locked by `tests/velocity-preset-layers.test.ts` (7 + 1 browser-skipped: data contract, bank resolvability, measured Goertzel anchor-dominance per zone, command apply/undo/idempotence, JSON migration; a naive FFT-peak estimator proved the wrong instrument — resonant sweeps pull the peak off the fundamental).
- **Kit humanize (2026-10-09)** — the drum-side completion: the new kits and the latin genre swap carried voices but no variant sets, and the kit-swap paths kept **stale layers under a swapped asset** (a pad re-voiced by a genre re-generation kept playing the previous kit's samples through its old pool). Now: **5 latin RR pools** in `RR_VARIATIONS` (shaker soft/fast, bongos, conga quinto, timbale — the martillo/güira/cascara cells are the most exposed machine-gun reads), the **latin genre swap lands the world-perc voices** (pad 12 → conga quinto, pad 13 → bongos, both with pools; güira shaker with its pool), **`KitPreset` pads carry `layers`** (Latin/Jazz/Rock kits ride the snare dynamic set, hat dynamics, kick punch RR and the perc pools through the dice swap), `resolveKitAssignments` guarantees emitted sets cover the emitted asset (otherwise `[]`), and the apply/preview/genre/kit-capture paths all honor or clear layers (DiceContext patch spread, GhostPreviewPlayer preview parity, `applyKitToDrumTrack` capture round-trip). Locked by `tests/kit-velocity-humanize.test.ts` (8/8: pool derivation bases, set resolvability + own-asset coverage across all kits, dice emission invariant, stale-layer regression, latin generation + idempotence).
- **Jazz + rock genre promotion (2026-10-09)** — before this wave a "jazz" prompt landed a HOUSE beat (the jazzy lanes rode house/boombap) and bare "rock" had no genre at all. Both are now first-class (19 → 21), complete per the promotion protocol: **grooves** (jazz owns the bebop/swing/big-band swing tradition — promoted out of the boombap lanes with `jazz.*` ids; rock ships Backbeat / Half-Time / Punk Drive, swing 0 by design — the grid IS the genre), **parser routing** with compound guards ("jazz rap" keeps boombap, "nu jazz" keeps house, "post-rock" keeps postrock, the metal family keeps its dedicated house.metal lanes — only the BARE words promote), **song forms** (jazz: the AABA head form with solo space; rock: the radio verse/pre-chorus/chorus form), **BPM defaults** (130/120), **kit colouring riding the humanize sets** (jazz: washey ride as THE spine + comping dynamic set; rock: punch-kick RR + snare dynamics + crash-roll fills), **feel** (jazz the played 0.12/0.14, rock the tight 0.04/0.09), **harmony** (ii-V-I / rhythm changes / minor ii-V-i; I-IV-V / I-V-vi-IV / minor descent), **style-quality profiles** (jazz: the widest syncopation tolerance in the library) and **measured references** (jazz −13.1 LUFS / PLR 11.9 — the most dynamic genre measured; rock −11.5 / 10.3). `Genre` is now SINGLE-SOURCE (`type Genre` derives from the `GENRES` const — a promotion edits one place); `tests/audio-targets.test.ts` imports the union instead of a rotting hand-list. Locked by `tests/genre-jazz-rock.test.ts` (8/8, the full promotion protocol).
- **Lead motif reprise + promoted-genre melody (2026-10-10)** — two full-song gaps closed: (1) **the chorus hook** — before, every section's lead was an independent dice roll, so a song had no signature you could hum; `applyLeadHook` makes the FIRST hook section (chorus/drop) that carries a lead the DONOR, and every later hook section replays THAT motif (repeated to its own length, tick-unit aware — NoteEvent times are ticks while section lengths are 16th steps), while verses and the bridge keep their generated contrast and honest-empty stays empty. Default ON; `hookReprise: false` is the A/B escape hatch. Runs before transitions carve section tails, on the mixed default AND every lane alternative. (2) **rock/jazz melodic tables** — `MELODIC_BY_GENRE` covered only 5 genres, so the promoted genres generated DRUMS-ONLY songs; rock now chugs root 8ths + power shapes + a signature pentatonic riff, jazz walks quarters + Charleston comping + loose 8th lines (literal `MelodicNote` arrays, velocities calibrated below house/trap). En route: the reference trim clamp was HARDCODED ±6 dB at the compute site (next to the `TRIM_LIMIT_DB` constant) — full-band rock/jazz measured ~7 dB above target and the cap left them the hottest references in the library; the clamp now uses the constant (passed into the browser evaluate) and the window is 9 dB. Locked by `tests/song-hook-reprise.test.ts` (5: donor/implant, wrap+trim at differing section lengths, honest-empty, E2E hook identity across choruses, A/B opt-out).
- **Fill vocabulary (2026-10-10)** — per-section drum fills that stopped being one shape: the fill/riser treatments painted the SAME hardcoded 16th-roll at every boundary regardless of what came next. Now a **6-recipe pool** (crescendo-roll, half-push, tom-cascade, ghost-turnaround, kick-drive, stop-tail) in two intensity tiers — the recipe is a SEEDED pick per boundary (keyed by the outgoing pattern id + the incoming label, so the same boundary always plays the same fill while a song's boundaries vary) and the INCOMING section's intensity gates the tier (a bridge turnaround reads subtle, a drop lands the big one). The riser's final bar rides the same pool; dropout (the silent gap), `allowDrums: false` and unknown types are untouched. Threaded from `applySongTransitions` (intensity + boundary key) through `applyTransitionToPattern`. Locked by `tests/song-fill-vocabulary.test.ts` (5: last-bar paint + surviving kick anchor + roll voices, seeded determinism + cross-boundary variety, the subtle-tier contract — no full-bar roll — with the big tier reaching it, dropout purity, role-request suppression).
- **Full-song progress telemetry (2026-10-10)** — the SUNO song build (the longest interactive wait in the product) reported sections as prose only; now `composeFullTrack`'s `onProgress` carries an optional STRUCTURED second argument (`{ done, total, label }` — the string stays the single source for text-only callers like MCP) and the IntentPanel renders a real **section progress bar** (aria progressbar with value/label — "3/7 · Chorus 1") under the status line while the build runs, torn down when the draft lands or the build fails. Locked by `tests/ui/IntentPanelSongProgress.test.tsx` (drives the REAL deterministic engine end-to-end: the bar appears mid-build with sane counter bounds and is gone with the button interactive again).
- **Intent mining→retraining cycle (2026-10-11)** — the loop closed as ONE command: `npm run intent:cycle` turns the panel's local mining export (`__kyxIntentFailures.export()` → `scripts/data/intent-mined.json`) into cycle artifacts — every unique clarify/model-miss CLASSIFIED against the current engine (`src/intent/mining-cycle.ts`, pure): RESOLVED rows (mined asks that today route to a real action) become the mined regression corpus the dataset generator appends as teacher pairs (pinning past failures' resolutions against regression), STILL-FAILING rows are the human work list, model-hits are wins. `--train` chains dataset → train → gate with honest per-step failure (the shipped artifact is never touched by a failed run). Mined prompts stay LOCAL (gitignored — the failure-log etiquette). En route two latent breaks fixed: `intent:dataset` never warmed the preset lazy seam (threw since the 2026-10-04 pack wave — the same cold-bank bug sat in the SFT golden harness) and the golden test now warms too. Locked by `tests/mining-cycle.test.ts` (5: classification, collapsed counts, purity, corpus sidecar, language guess).
- **Harmony-first generation (2026-10-10, the intent engine's biggest fix)** — the multi-voice engine (chord progressions + voice leading) was reachable ONLY from the never-invoked `SymbolicPriorProvider.generate` branch, so EVERY real generation (dice, standalone patterns, song sections) played Markov templates that knew nothing about the progression tables — and the song-level harmony plan had no living consumer. `generatePattern` now routes melodic roles through `generateMultiVoice` as the PRIMARY source (all-or-nothing per candidate — bass/chords/lead are one harmonic decision, never mixed across engines), with the Markov templates as fallback and the role scope (dice drums+bass, "no lead" briefs) cutting across the engine exactly like it cut across the templates. The song harmony plan (`options.progression`) is LIVE: different plans produce different chords on the same seed. Side effect: the 14 genres without `MELODIC_BY_GENRE` tables now generate melody on the default path (the engine works from the per-genre progression tables). Locked by `tests/harmony-first-generation.test.ts` (3: plan reaches the default path with determinism, the previously melody-less genres sing, role scope).
- **Melodic table coverage 21/21 + dialect precedence (2026-10-11)** — the 14 engine-only genres (drill, phonk, jersey, hyperpop, ukg, boombap, amapiano, trance, detroit, postrock, chiptune, eurodance, latin, drone) now carry their own `MELODIC_BY_GENRE` identity tables (literal `MelodicNote` arrays): the drill slide, the amapiano log drum's grid-dodging wide intervals, jersey's bounce, ukg's downbeat-dodging 2-step, chiptune's 1-step arp chains, eurodance's classic off-beat octave bass, the tumbao anticipation, post-rock's whole-note pads, drone's single breath. The tables pay TWICE — template fallback AND multi-voice reads their `octaveOffset` (the engine's register per genre). **Precedence fixed: a matched style DIALECT (38 in `MELODIC_BY_STYLE`) now REPLACES the harmony engine instead of being buried under it** (a dialect is an explicit identity statement; `hasMelodicDialect` gates the choice in `generatePattern`) — the harmony-first wave had accidentally made the dialects unreachable. Colliding constant names with the dialects renamed (AMAPIANO_LOG__, DRILL_SLIDE__, JERSEY_BOUNCE__, UKG_TWO_STEP__). Locked by `tests/melodic-table-coverage.test.ts` (4: 21/21 structured coverage, distinct octave identities, dialect-key matching, dialect-replaces-engine through `generatePattern`).
- **Section development engine (2026-10-12, W1 of the vocal campaign)** — a repeated section used to roll new dice; now it EVOLVES deterministically. `applySectionDevelopment` (pure, after the hook reprise, before transitions) applies three treatments keyed off repetition index and song position: **hook quotation** (later verses open with the chorus hook's first fragment at half velocity — the verse remembers), **final lift** (the LAST hook section lifts its drum rows' second half +0.08), **pre-final dip** (a bridge/build directly before it dips to half — the quiet before the last hit). Treatments COMPOSE (dipped bridge beside lifted final chorus); first occurrences stay baseline; `development: false` = flat A/B. Locked by `tests/section-development.test.ts` (4).
- **Lyrics→prosody (2026-10-12, W2)** — `src/vocal/prosody.ts`: text → syllables (vowel-group split, EN+SK, deterministic approximations) → a SINGABLE grid (2 bars/line; stressed syllables ON the beat at the head with durational accent, unstressed ride 8ths, the line tail holds, line breaks breathe) → `shapeMelodyToLyrics` keeps the melody's PITCH language and adopts the grid's TIME — the lyrics' rhythm becomes the melody's rhythm without inventing pitches. The W3 hum→voice wave sings these grids. Locked by `tests/prosody.test.ts` (6: pinned real splits, blank-line breaths, beat-head stress, tail holds, pitch-language preservation, honest-empty).
- **Hum→voice (2026-10-12, W3 of the vocal campaign)** — "tvoj hlas, tvoja skladba": `src/vocal/voiceify.ts` turns the user's own hum recording into a tuned lead. The engine estimates the hum's pitch ONCE (autocorrelation, two-pass: local maxima scoring ≥ 0.8 of best, smallest = the fundamental, parabolic interpolation — the global-max form lands on period multiples and a threshold picks peak shoulders, both classic octave/±100-cent errors), pre-crossfades the sustained region into a seamless loop (wraps read as vibrato), and per target note reads the loop at ratio 2^((target−source)/12) with 5 ms note-edge fades. The result is the user's TIMBRE singing the melody's pitches — the thing SUNO structurally cannot offer. Locked by `tests/voiceify.test.ts` (6: autocorrelation lands on the known tone, harmonic-hum fixture, target-pitch render via zero-crossing, tick→sample timing with silent gaps, honest-empty, determinism). `applyVoiceLeadCommand` (adapt.ts) wires it into the product: the staged voiceify render becomes a 'Voice Lead — hum' vocalchop track playing the target melody's notes — one undoable snapshot, re-apply reuses the slot with sampleId refreshed to the new render. Locked by `tests/voice-lead.test.ts` (4: install+undo, reuse with sampleId refresh, honest refusals, voiceify+command integration).
- **Intent model browser route verified (refreshed 2026-10-14)** — the active v17 dynamic-INT8 artifact (2,601,522 bytes; 6,144 input features; 59 heads, 26 kind classes) registers `ready` in Chromium. Validation is 287/297 attempted-exact (96.6%), zero wrong-kind, 77/374 abstentions (20.6%); the gate permits up to 23%. Golden is 71/71 attempted-exact, zero wrong-kind, six abstentions, with 68/77 prompts overlapping training. Current Chromium measured ~1,200 ms cold load and ~29 ms smoke inference. A separate 144-row assistant-authored English diagnostic, with zero normalized exact-prompt overlap, measured combined 71/72 actions, 36/36 clarifications, 36/36 safe abstentions, and zero false-positive actions. One provisional action label (“Raise the lead delay return”) is clarified because it can refer to either a send or a return. This is deployment plus a synthetic diagnostic, not evidence of broad independent generalization or musical quality.
- **crash.pop re-voice (library-gap wave)** — the shipped seed was the crash.main recipe on a lower band (feature distance 0.025 — a duplicate, and darker than main despite the "Bright, Airy" manifest). Re-voiced as the short bright pop splash: air-forward HPF body + metallic definition ping + sparkle tail (centroid 6.2 → 8.3 kHz, decay 292 → 162 ms, distance to crash.main 0.301).
- **Vinyl crackle texture (library-gap wave)** — `factory.fx.vinyl` (97 → 98 assets): the lo-fi/phonk surface bed the bank lacked — seeded dust ticks + rare pops over a hiss floor, 4 s flat/stationary loop (wrap drift below mid-file sample variance), deliberately mid/treble only (300 Hz HPF, sub at −37 dB rel) so the 808 keeps the floor.
- **Sub-drop FX (library-gap wave)** — `factory.fx.subdrop` (96 → 97 assets): the pitch-falling sub the riser/downlifter family lacked — two-octave sine fall (C3 → semitone-clean C1) landing loud then fading, mild tanh edge + falling band-passed air; runs at near-unity tape drive in the seed chain (the sustained-sub squaring lesson). The `drop` transition cue now fires impact + sub-drop (was impact + downlifter).
- **Long open-hat wash (library-gap wave)** — `factory.hat.wash` (95 → 96 assets): the only bank hat past 100 ms decay (inharmonic ping bank + ~0.9 s darkening noise tail, audible ring ~250 ms) so a 4/4 offbeat can bloom instead of tick; leads the trance kit offbeat mask with its own RR pool.
- **Room snare (library-gap wave) + mastering convergence** — `factory.snare.room` (94 → 95 assets): the only bank snare with a tail (dry crack + 3 early-reflection taps + dark diffuse tail, HPF'd at 240 Hz so the room adds depth not mud); leads the boom-bap kit backbeat with its own RR pool. The same wave fixed the curated-seed mastering chain (tape driveGain squared sustained sources past the loudness loop — 808s shipped up to 5.8 dB hot): linear post-master trim to the exact category target, content-DC removal, limiter look-ahead lead trim, per-asset tape-drive override (808pure stays Clean), and 808 glide rests snapped to semitones (C#1/D1/D#1/E1). All 92 WAVs re-rendered; per-category loudness spread ≤ 0.8 dB. _(sound-library audit 2026-09-29)_
- **Pad round-robin + velocity dynamics** — `DrumPad.layers` (schema v8) with per-pad variant sets resolved in the shared groove plan (live == offline): stock kit gets snare/closed-hat DYNAMIC sets (ghost → body pool → accent bands), drill/phonk/jersey/dnb kits carry their own RR pools (snare.drill/phonk/jersey/dnb, kick.drill/dnb/jersey/phonk, hat.drill/phonk/jersey/dnb); `factory.kick.sub` typo fixed to `sub808`. Factory bank re-derives variants after the curated layer overrides a base (same drum, micro-variation — not two alternating sounds).
- **Phase-preserving audio-loop splits** — schema v9 adds optional `AudioClip.loopPhaseOffsetSec`; splitting a looped clip keeps the same trimmed source region and advances the right fragment's playback phase without baking or duplicating audio.
- **Project-local Producer Brief** — schema v10 persists only explicitly saved, allowlisted creative facts with source/confidence metadata; legacy projects migrate without inventing a brief, while prompt text, audio and candidate payloads remain outside the stored field. The user can inspect provenance, opt in/out per session, forget one fact or clear the whole brief with undo; project isolation, generation defaults, IndexedDB persistence and Y.Doc round-trips are covered by targeted tests.
- **Genre feel (humanize defaults)** — generated songs apply a per-genre humanize pocket (`applyGenreFeelToDoc`: trap 0.06/0.12, drill 0.05/0.10, phonk 0.12/0.16, jersey 0.05/0.10, house 0.08/0.12, techno 0.04/0.08, dnb 0.06/0.14, ambient 0.15/0.10; timing/velocity) in the same undo step as the kit swap; swing is never written (owned by the generation path) and a user-set non-zero humanize always wins.
- **De-click guarantee** — every one-shot gets a 2 ms tail (`DECLICK_TAIL_SEC`) on the trigger, sliced-preview and preview-stop paths; looped pads fade on the 30 s safety stop instead of the slice end.
- **Artifact gate** (`src/audio-engine/artifactGate.ts`) — pure numerical defect analysis over Float32Arrays (runs in vitest AND the real-browser verifier): non-finite samples, hard tail cut (measured as the last audible sample vs peak, so a silent tail is fine), isolated discontinuities via curvature residual vs the local body (the intentional attack is excluded), opt-in sub-audio DC ceiling, true-peak ceiling, and `logEnvelopeCorrelation` for round-robin spread (sample-phase correlation collapses on a 1.018 resample; the log envelope is phase-blind). Browser gate renders 8 genres + a p-locked length cut and asserts consecutive hat hits are not the same sample.
- **Bass pack (library-completion wave, 2026-10-04)** — the empty category, filled with six D2/D3-anchored one-shots: `bass.clean` (sine sub), `bass.reese` (detuned saws + slow LFO), `bass.fm` (3.5× inharmonic modulator), `bass.pluck` (D3 house bounce), `bass.wobble` (6.2 Hz resonant LFO growl), `bass.dist` (tanh-saturated mid-forward). New `Bass` `AssetCategory` joins the sampler-side browser list and kit colours; seed target −12 LUFS at near-unity tape drive (the sustained-sub lesson). Locked by `tests/bass-pack.test.ts` (registry coherence + FFT tuning anchor). _(111-curated contract)_
- **Kick trio de-dup (2026-10-04)** — `pop`/`punch`/`jersey` re-voiced as a three-role ladder (G#1 neutral / A1 tight pop / B1 jersey high-bounce, click and decay separating them — pair fell out of the top-8 nearest neighbours); `soft`/`trap` separated on the two non-spectral features (long click-light boom vs short hard-clicked wide pitch-drop, crest 5.1 → 6.0). Mallet strike path calibrated: contact band-pass gain compensated so `clickLevel` means the strike's level at the contact, per-voice bands/levels led by each contact's own envelope (vibes −20.6 → −0.2 dB, musicbox +4.3 dB). All on their category targets.
- **Transition pack (2026-10-04)** — three quick siblings of the long family: `fx.impact2` (cinematic metal-ring hit), `fx.riser-short` (0.7 s cliff), `fx.noise-down` (0.8 s falling air). On the −13 LUFS FX target; ABX lanes added (impact2-vs-impact, riser-short-vs-riser, noisedown-vs-downlifter).
- **Percussion flavor pack (2026-10-04)** — `perc.woodblock` (two wooden cavity modes), `perc.clave` (dry 3-2 son click, <40 ms), `perc.snapstack` (three staggered finger snaps), `shaker.long` (0.4 s swish tail for ballad 16ths). On the −13.5 LUFS Percussion target; wooden/clicky gap between conga and tick filled.
- **The curated layer loads DETERMINISTICALLY and completely (2026-10-22)** — two defects, both in the same loader, both visible as "the same project exported twice sounds different". (1) The whole-layer byte ceiling was **30 MB while the shipped layer is 35.5 MB of WAV** — so ~15 % of the product's own masters were silently truncated on every boot and the slots that fell back to the synth were whatever the loader happened to reach. (2) The bytes were counted in **fetch-COMPLETION order**, so which slots crossed the ceiling depended on which download answered first: 17 skipped in one run, 15 in the next. The ceiling now covers the shipped layer (40 MiB) and the accounting runs in the **canonical sample order** (the fetch still runs in parallel; only the accounting is ordered), so a future layer that does exceed the ceiling truncates deterministically instead of randomly. Locked by `tests/curated-samples.test.ts` (11 specs — the truncation must pick the same slots whichever download answers first, and the shipped layer must fit under the ceiling; both verified with a mutation, not just by reading).
- **The round-robin check stopped manufacturing its own defect (2026-10-25)** — the browser verifier answered "is this round-robin variant the same drum as its base?" with SAMPLE-PHASE correlation and a 0.7 threshold, and that metric collapses on a resampled copy by construction — the repository's own artifact gate says so in prose ("a round-robin variant is a resampled copy (rate 1.018), so by the end of a 150 ms hit the phase has drifted ~180° and sample correlation collapses to ~0.1"). Measured on the real bank (26 round-robin bases, in a browser, against a clean baseline checkout): **10 of 26 bases failed the threshold on the SYNTH bank alone — before the curated layer it was written to police had even loaded** — and 19 of 26 failed after it; broadband sounds (hats, shakers) read −0.05…0.02, kicks read 0.86…0.99. A gate that can only go red carries no information. The check now uses the phase-blind `logEnvelopeCorrelation` the codebase already ships (`src/sample-library/rrSimilarity.ts`): **0 of 26 fail, minimum 0.99996**. The replacement is not a rubber stamp — a stale variant (one derived from a base the bank no longer holds) measures **0.696–0.975**, and **22 of 24** such real pairs fall below the 0.95 threshold. Stated limit: the metric separates a different CHARACTER (a mastered recording against a synth fallback), not the same character rendered differently, so two hits differing only in their decay constant stay above the threshold (0.998). Locked by `tests/rr-variant-similarity.test.ts` (9 specs, including the negative controls that prove the guard can still go red).
- **The factory catalogue is built OFF the boot path (2026-10-25)** — `generateFactoryBank()` rendered all 155 factory assets inside `createCoreServices()`, which IS the studio boot: measured **650 ms boot against a 734 ms catalogue** — 113 % of the TTI budget — and the curated WAV layer alone needs 115 s cold, so the catalogue cannot be skipped, only MOVED. It now renders in the BACKGROUND (`startFactoryBankBuild`), the boot returns in **15 ms**, opening a project awaits exactly the samples that project uses (`ensureFactorySamples` + `factorySampleIdsOf`: 16 ids → 30 ms, 16/16 present), and an export awaits the whole catalogue **without a timeout** (a silent export would be worse than a slow one). Measured on the way there: creating the 155 OfflineAudioContexts is **12 %** of the cost and the RENDER is **88 %**, and ONE context rendering all 155 sounds is SLOWER (2309 ms) than 155 contexts (774 ms) — "fewer contexts" was a dead hypothesis. Two side findings: the curated layer's 30 MB byte ceiling truncated the load, so the SKIPPED ids differed between runs (17 vs 15) and the fetch order was not deterministic — **FIXED** (40 MiB ceiling + canonical byte accounting, locked by `tests/curated-samples.test.ts`); and the synthetic render is **reproducible only to within ONE ULP of float32** — measured by rendering the whole catalogue TWICE in one page and comparing the buffers byte for byte: 43 of 207 assets differ, worst sample delta **1.5e-6 (−116 dBFS)**. The first hypothesis (an unseeded RNG) was WRONG: every noise source on that path is SEEDED (`noiseBuffer(ctx, hashString(track.id) ^ 0xd42d)`, `makeNoise(ctx, seconds, seed)` over `mulberry32`), and the per-sample deltas are exact powers of two (2^-20, 2^-24, 2^-25, 2^-27) — the fingerprint of a ONE-ULP difference, not of a different random stream. A second measurement pinned the mechanism: the differing samples are on LOUD values (0.03 … 1.0, e.g. `clap.main` at 0.027, `snare.room` at 0.476), not in a quiet tail; the differing assets are exactly those whose builders run a **BiquadFilter** (clap, snare, hat, ride, crash, cowbell) while the oscillator-only control (`factory.kick.deep`) comes out **bit-identical**. Chromium's biquad is therefore not bit-reproducible across render instances, and the IIR state amplifies the first-bit difference to a few ULP. The old wording (“the phase correlation of the same pair drifts by ~1e-9”) described the CORRELATION METRIC moving on top of that drift — it is ill-conditioned exactly where the correlation is near zero (hats ≈ 0.000, snares ≈ 0.5) — and understated it: the buffers themselves move, by an amount that is inaudible and ~6 orders of magnitude below the loudness gate's 2.5 dB tolerance. The browser gate now PINS that magnitude (`factory catalogue renders reproducibly (≤1 ULP: Chromium's biquad rounding)`: max delta ≤ 2e-6 across the six filter-driven assets and the oscillator-only control exactly 0), so a real regression — an unseeded noise source, a reordered builder — moves the delta by five orders of magnitude and turns the gate red. Locked by `tests/factory-bank-deferred.test.ts` (10 specs: the boot does not wait, the project open does, the export waits for the whole catalogue AND for a render still in flight, a second request for an in-flight asset joins it instead of rendering it twice, and every place that reads the whole bank — the mastering copy, the audio index, the portable-project install — waits for the catalogue first).

### Preset expansion

- Synth presets 199 → 220 — 21 genre-anchored synth voices (analog leads / stabs / pads, wavetable morph leads, fm bells / keys, club plucks). _(feat `7af7c0d`)_
- Drum presets 220 → 232 — 12 genre-anchored drum-synth voices (drill crackers, memphis snare, dusty tom, jersey clap, dnb snare / rim / open cup, first Rimshot type). _(feat `027dcab`)_
- 808 presets 232 → 242 — 10 genre-anchored 808 voices with `GLIDE` front and center for drill / phonk slide ladder. _(feat `2ba6a9d`)_
- **808 MASSIVE engine** — the 808 synth gains the modern-trap beater stack: `PUNCH` (pitch envelope +0–24 st above the target, `P-TIME` ramp 5–150 ms — kicks instead of fading), `KNOCK` (short 2.5× sine beater through the shaper), `GRIT` (square at the fundamental wired pre-shaper so DRIVE/DIST generate phone-audible 200–400 Hz harmonics; glides with slides), and the DIST ladder grows `TAPE` (smooth no-plateau saturation) + `FOLD` (wavefolder grind). 11 new presets (Metro Knock, Phone 808, Drill Knock, Folded Drill, Phonk Fold, Tape Boom, Cube Clean, Cinematic Drop, Jersey Punch, Grit Sub, Slide Lesson) take 808 voices 25 → 36, all measured into the loudness map. Locked by `tests/808-massive.test.ts` (11 wiring contracts).
- Browser factory preset audio QA currently green at 252/252 (after Flute preset addition).
- Pop preset pack — 24 vocal-first presets (bright keys/plucks, lush/bedroom/air pads, marimba/celesta/nylon/rhodes/wurli/sad-piano carriers, round basses, tuned 808s, soft leads, FM bells), all measured into the loudness map.
- Pop preset delta — 7 fill-in voices: intimate upright piano, kalimba + music box (consuming the two orphaned mallet assets), funky moving bass, dance-pop saw lead, FM DX-style piano, vocal-chop pop adlib; tonal/mallet factory assets now all have a preset consumer. _(feat pop preset delta)_

### Intent and AI

- **AI vrstva hovorí dokumentovým metrom (ADR 0057 Stage 2b, 2026-10-21)** — generátor bol posledná vrstva, ktorá si myslela, že jeden takt má vždy 16 šestnástiniek: `bars = Math.ceil(stepCount / 16)`, fráza sa strihala na `bar * 16`, kick bol na `step % 4 === 0`, snare na `step % 8 === 4` a groove referencia sa skladala na `step % 16`. Zmerané pred zmenou: v 3/4 dokumente generátor **neodmietol** vzor — vyrobil 4/4 takt useknutý na 12. kroku, takže **beat-4 backbeat bol proste preč** (kick 0/4/8, snare len 4), a v 6/8 ten istý useknutý 4/4 takt tvrdil, že je shuffle. Nový vlastník `src/ai/meter.ts` drží metrické fakty AI vrstvy (dĺžka taktu, pulz, pravidlo kotiev, fáza referencie) a **neprepisuje si vzor** — `stepsPerBarFor`/`stepsPerBeatFor` prichádzajú z `src/project-model/grid.ts`. Meter cestuje v `GenerateOptions.timeSignature` a v recepte (`PatternGeneration.timeSignature`) **LEN ak nie je 4/4**, takže 4/4 si ponechá rovnaký obsahový hash, rovnaké kotvy aj recept: negatívna kontrola v `tests/adr0057-ai-meter.test.ts` je pinutá na hashy namerané pred st stage (`17e1439d` house, `688279c1` trap, `47f20c59` dnb, `b4f0ae78` house-2). Kotvy idú podľa metra: 4/4 kick 0/4/8/12 + snare 4/12, 3/4 kick 0/4/8 + snare 4, **6/8 kick 0/6 + snare 2/8** (šampiónový "2 & 5", nie tresillo), 7/8 = nepravidelný takt = len downbeat (skupina je OBSAH). Referencia groove sa skladá na metrickom taktu, takže druhý takt vzoru číta znova prvý takt groove. Brána: tretí guard (`src/ai/` nesmie merať takt ako 16 krokov; deklarované výnimky = Markovov stavový priestor, tréningový frame korpusu, pinované ONNX feature framy, v2 kontrakt) so syntetickou poruškou, ktorá dokazuje, že guard vie zlyhať. 18 špecov v `tests/adr0057-ai-meter.test.ts`. **Otvorené (deklarované):** groove OBSAH je stále 4/4 (Markovov model má 16 pozícií, ONNX priory pinujú 16-krokový takt) — to je Stage 2c; assist vrstva (`src/assist/patternOps.ts`) si zatiaľ bary počíta sama, lebo jej groove šablóny sú 4/4 obsah.

- **Groove OBSAH hovorí dokumentovým metrom (ADR 0057 Stage 2c, 2026-10-21)** — knižnica je 4/4 materiál (258 grooveov, každý riadok 16 šestnástiniek) a Stage 2b ju čítal na metrickom taktu: polovica materiálu padla ZA koniec taktu — UKG clap na „and" of 3 a „and" of 4 (step 12 a 14) je za koncom 12-krokového 6/8 taktu, trap kick na „and" of 4 v 3/4 proste nebol. Nový vlastník `src/ai/groove-view.ts` je **metrický POHĽAD** na groove (odvodená projekcia, nie druhá knižnica): základ sa poskladá do taktu dokumentu, kotvové role (kick, snare, clap) sa prehodia na metrickú kostru, materiál sa zbiera z CELÉHO zdrojového riadka (4/4 beat-4 backbeat žije za koncom 3/4 taktu — a práve on je materiál, ktorý kratší takt potrebuje), a nepravidelný takt (7/8, 5/8) si svoje skupovanie NECHTÁ zobaliť (skupina je OBSAH, knižnica nemá 7/8 groovey). Zmerané proti rodičovskému commitu (worktree, rovnaký skript, rovnaké seedy): kotvové hity mimo kostry klesli v 3/4 z **28,0 % na 5,9 %** a v 5/4 z **25,9 % na 6,0 %**, zatiaľ čo 4/4 si ponechal **tých istých 248 hitov a tých istých 97 mimo kostry** — štýlová synkopácia je OBSAH, nie kontaminácia. Kotva v 6/8 je na šampiónových „2 & 5" a namiesto „najbližšieho suseda" si nesie vlastnú hlasitosť groove (ukg.ukg v 6/8: kick 6: 0,41 → **0,73**, clap 2: 0,45 → **0,60**). 4/4 je **bitovo identický** (tie isté štyri hashe `17e1439d` / `688279c1` / `47f20c59` / `b4f0ae78`) a brána je silnejšia ako štyri hashe: **metrický pohľad je IDENTITYTA pre všetkých 258 grooveov v 4/4** (rovnaké pozície, rovnaké hodnoty, rovnaká dĺžka). 27 špecov v `tests/adr0057-metrical-groove.test.ts`. **Otvorené (deklarované):** pinované 16-krokové framy ONNX priorov a v2 kontraktu — to je Stage 2e (retrain + nové manifesty, nie preklopenie); Markovov model si svoj frame ponechá a projekcia mu ho preloží.

- **Metrum je SPÝTANÉ (ADR 0057 Stage 2f, 2026-10-21)** — vrstvy nad taktom hovorili dokumentovým metrom, ale REQUEST nie: takt bol dostupný len ako doslovná fráza „time signature 3/4", čo je API pravopis, nie ľudský jazyk. „make me a beat in 6/8" vyrobil 4/4 beat v 4/4 projekte — takt bol ticho ignorovaný, najhorší druh zlyhania: žiadna chyba, len beat v nesprávnom takte. Nový vlastník `src/intent/meter-phrase.ts` je **jediný slovník metra** a oba parsery (text aj exact) sa ho pýtajú — dva parsery tej istej vety nesmú o metre rozporovať. Slovník je zámerne úzky: pomenovaný tvar („time signature 3/4", „meter 6/8", „takt 7/8", „signatura 5/4"), pomenovaný pocit („waltz"/„valčík" = 3/4) a **holý tvar („6/8") len v generovace** — command cesta ho nesmie vziať („make me a 3/4 beat" by zmenilo dokument bez beatu). **„shuffle" NIE JE takt:** UKG shuffle je 4/4 so swung šestnástinkou, pocit nie bar. **Poradok je podstata:** veta môže pomenovať takt aj dĺžku („2 bars in 3/4") a dĺžka sa číta v dokumente, takže metrum sa aplikuje **prvé** (stable sort) — inak „2 bars in 3/4" pridá 32 krokov namiesto 24. Požadovaný takt zároveň rozhoduje o dĺžke (`intent.timeSignature ?? doc.timeSignature`) a do dokumentu sa dostáva len vtedy, keď ho používateľ pomenoval (12 krokov v 6/8 taktu = presne jeden takt). 4/4 je **bitovo identický** (rovnaký intent hash, rovnaký vygenerovaný beat, rovnaké naučené preferencie), 11 špecov v `tests/adr0057-requested-meter.test.ts`.

- **Assist vrstva počíta takty v dokumente (ADR 0057 Stage 2g, 2026-10-21)** — assist bola posledná vrstva, ktorá si myslela, že takt má 16 šestnástiniek: `baseBars = round(stepCount/16)`, `stepCount = bars*16`, zóna fillu `stepCount - 16`, štýlové groovey štiepené po 16 krokoch a „beat" ako `i % 4 === 0`. Zmerané v 6/8 dokumente pred zmenou: štyr-taktový BUILD = 64 krokov = **5,33 taktu**, zóna fillu začínala cez bar line, „four-on-floor" kick jazdil na ôsmych (0/4/8/12) a thinning vedel **zmažať backbeat** (16 z 200 seedov). Teraz sa takt **NEDERIVUJE**: `src/assist/patternOps.ts` sa pýta `src/ai/meter.ts` (pulz, backbeat, dĺžka taktu) a `src/ai/groove-view.ts` (ako 4/4 materiál hovorí v inom takte); každá operácia má meter ako **posledný** argument s defaultom `DEFAULT_AI_METER`, takže 4/4 ostáva byte-identický — 8 z 8 operácií (VARY, BUILD, REPLACE hats/kicks/snares, FILL, thinning) je **bitovo identických** proti pre-patch implementácii v tom istom procese, na rovnakom fixture, s rovnakými seedmi. Kotvy sú metrické (pulz a backbeat), nie „každý štvrtý krok": v 6/8 je to 0/6 a 2/8, takže thinning **nemôže** zmažať backbeat (0 z 200 seedov). Štýlové groovey sa **prehodzujú**, neorežúvajú: four-on-floor → 0/6/12/18, backbeat → 2/8/14/20, house hats si nechajú preložené pozície (2/6/10). Zdroj, ktorý nenapĺňa celé takt, sa **oreže na bar line** a rez sa **POČÍTA a HOVORÍ** (`assistSourceAlignment`, `Command.detail`, preview) — rez, ktorý nevidíš, je rez, ktorý nevieš undo-núť. Preview assistu kreslí jeden **metrický takt** (12 buniek v 6/8) s metrickými beat značkami a ledger viditeľných 4/4 miest sa **zmenšil**. 14 špecov v `tests/adr0057-assist-meter.test.ts`.

- **Metrum sa nikdy nemení potichu (ADR 0057 Stage 2h, 2026-10-21)** — ADR 0056 odkazoval tri plochy, ktoré menili metrum bez disclosure, a štvrtá (AI generovanie) vôbec nebola v zozname, lebo `src/commands/aiPattern.ts` píše `doc.timeSignature` priamo a nikdy neprejde cez príkaz. Dnes sú štyri a všetky vyzývajú JEDNÉHO vlastníka slov, `src/project-model/meter-disclosure.ts`: príkaz (`Command.detail` → toast), intent read-back, odpoveď MCP a AI cesta. Veta meria fakty, nie pocity: takt v tikoch a krokoch, **pulz v tikoch a krokoch** a koľko pulzov takt má, plus čo sa stane s patternsom používateľa. **Pravidlo ticha je PODPIS, nie takt:** 4/4 → 2/2 si takt 1920 tikov ponechá, ale pulz sa zmení na polnú (metronóm kliká 2× namiesto 4×), takže to je zmeniteľné a musí sa oznámiť; 4/4 → 4/4 nehovorí vôbec nič. 24 špecov v `tests/adr0057-meter-disclosure.test.ts` — guard nájde každé miesto, ktoré staví metrum, a ledger môže len rásť menej. Retrain modelov ostáva to-do (nemáme metrické tréningové dáta).

- **Nepravidelný takt má SKUPINY, nie pulz (ADR 0057 Stage 2i, 2026-10-22)** — 7/8 a 5/8 mali vlastný režim: celý takt bol JEDEN pulz, kotvou bol len downbeat a skupovanie bolo „obsah, nie aritmetika". Bolo to správne aritmeticky a nesprávne dôsledne. Zmerané pred zmenou (house, seed „measure", dva takty, produktové kanónické optiony): 7/8 dokument vyrobil Kick Deep **[0, 14]** a Kick Punch **[0, 14]** — teda **samotný klik na beat 1 každého taktu** (plus pár osamelých ťapov); 5/8 vyrobil tiež len **[0, 10]**. Příčina bola v dvoch pravidlách kódu: (1) `enforceSyncopationBudget` maže každý hit, ktorého váha synkopácie nie je nula a ktorý nie je kotvou, a `syncopationWeight` považovala za „na mrežke" len `position % pulseSteps === 0` — v 7/8 je pulz celý takt, takže **každý hit mimo downbeatu vážil 1** (maximum) a rozpočet groove vypral; (2) `src/ai/groove-view.ts` odmietal prehlasovať kick v takte s jedným pulzom. Teraz `src/ai/meter.ts` nesie **SKUPINY**: 7/8 je **2+2+3**, 5/8 je **3+2** (deklarované, nie hádané — podpis sám nehovorí, ktoré skupovanie, a knižnica je 4/4: 258 grooveov po 16 krokoch), kick jazdí na **skupinových downbeatoch** (7/8: „1", „3", „5"), backbeat sedí na **akcente** (7/8: „5"; 5/8: „4") a synkopačná mrežka sleduje skupinu (ôsminy V ŠUKEJ skupine, nie v takte). Zmerané po zmene (ten istý skript a seed): 7/8 Kick Deep **[0, 4, 8, 14, 18, 22]** + Clap **[8, 22]** (akcent „5") + ghost 23, 5/8 Kick Deep **[0, 6, 10, 16]** + Clap **[6, 16]** (akcent „4") + ghost 5 — teda skutočný 7/8 groove namiesto kliku. **4/4, 6/8 aj nedeclarovaný 11/8 sú po lane identické** (11/8 Kick [0, 22], Clap [12] pred aj po). Pulz **zostáva** 14 krokov (to je fakt „pulz sa neopakuje"; skupiny hovoria, kde sú akcenty) a nedeclarovaný nepravidelný takt (11/8) si ponechá **celý takt ako jednu skupinu** — produkt neháda skupovanie, ktoré nepozná (11/8 kick [0, 22] pred aj po). 4/4 a každý pravidelný takt sú **bitovo identické** (tie isté štyri hashe `17e1439d` / `688279c1` / `47f20c59` / `b4f0ae78`), lebo pre jednoduchý takt sú skupiny presne pulzy. 19 špecov v `tests/adr0057-irregular-metre.test.ts`. **Otvorené (deklarované):** pinované ONNX framy sa NEMENIA (pôvodný model je stále 4/4-trained a irregularná cesta nemá tréningové dáta — to je ten istý to-do ako Stage 2e: `features.v3` + nové manifesty) a **groove knižnica je stále 4/4** — 7/8 groove robí metrický POHĽAD, nie knižnicová položka.

- **IntentPanel dekompozícia (F4, 2026-10-12)** — `src/ui/IntentPanel.tsx` **6 296 → 5 666 riadkov** (−630) bez jedinej zmeny správania. Šesť collaboratorov v `src/ui/intent/`: `SongDraftPanel` (646 — ♪ SONG draft, lanes, per-section A/B, loudness, audio review, revise; aj typy `SongDraft`/`SongPreferenceTake`/`SectionPreferenceTake`/`SongPreferenceRank` a helpery `sectionPreferenceOptions`/`sectionCompareSelection`), `CandidateBank` (124 — audition/★/USE/PRIOR-TPL/AUDIOTOOL), `VoiceIdeaControls` (57 — 🎶/🔊/🎤, vracia **fragment** lebo sú susedia v jednom control row), `ProducerSessionHud` (51 — 🎛 session chip + B/C varianty + reset), `AudioRefChip` (46 — 🎧 ref/match-EQ/clear), `StatusLine` (24 — 🤖⚡ router + osobný status, jeden komponent na dva call site-y). Fasáda `export function IntentPanel()` je **nezmenená** (žiadne props, žiadny pridaný export). Vyextrahované komponenty sú čisté renderery: 0 hookov, 0 audio uzlov, 0 mutácií — stav a všetky commandy ostali v shelli (React = renderer, AGENTS.md §3). Dve cesty prešli do shella, nie do workarounds: A/B porovnanie si nesie celé defaultované `{a,b}` (pôvodne sa spreadovala `selection`, nie `current[key]` — inak by B dropdown dostal prázdnu hodnotu) a AUDIOTOOL stale-preview guard (`previewDocRef` vs živý doc v `services.store`). Brány: boot path **2 114 → 2 116 KB** / 2 200 (bez regressie), DAW JS 5 150 → 5 155 KB, `typecheck` exit 0, `format:check` OK, `drift:check` ✓ no drift, `ai:performance` mediany 2.70/3.08/4.27 ms proti budgetu 250 ms. Testy: cielená 9-súborová brána **496/496** po každom commite; celá intent rodina (142 súborov / 1 973 testov) má **17 červených — identicky podľa názvu s baseline `2c2329fc`** (model pin/dataset schema, symbolic+melodic priory, preset loudness, master decisions, preference evaluation; cudzie vlny, `drift:bless` sa neriešil).

- **Audio rerank targets 19/19 genres (W0.1, killer-feature plan)** — D1 closed: the candidate sound rerank previously scored 15 of 19 genres against the hand-written house profile. `scripts/measure-genre-references.mjs` now also measures RMS / crest / ZCR / low-band-ratio on the full reference renders (mono downmix at 44100, the same convention as the runtime scoring path) and generates `src/intent/audio-targets.generated.ts` — corridors widened from measured medians (rms/zcr/bass ×[1/2.5, ×2.5], crest [max(1.2, m−4), m+8]). `audioTargetFor()` resolves measured → hand-written → house; locked by `tests/audio-targets.test.ts` (19/19 coverage, no house fallback for union members, corridor-center scores 1.0 on its own target).
- **Intent failure mining + training levers (library-gate wave)** — the ONNX intent head (multi-head slot-filling student of the deterministic layer) was re-grounded against the current corpus after the artifact had gone stale (attempted-exact 20.2 %, abstain 59 %), then pulled up with two measured levers: balanced positive weighting on the sigmoid multi-label heads (targets/pads collapsed to all-False under unweighted BCE — fixed, 14.9 → 35.6 %) and sqrt-inverse-frequency class CE on the long-tailed kind head (kind accuracy 0.870 → 0.906). Corpus gained a 71-pair mining wave (paraphrase siblings of the measured confusion families, appended at the corpus end so the every-7th val split keeps its rows; golden untouched), then **n-gram featurization v2** (word bigrams + fastText-style char 3-grams, `intent-features.v2`, vocab 2474, model 4.3 MB) — landing identically in the python trainer and the TS `buildIntentBow` (drift-gated): attempted-exact **20.2 → 77.0 %** (3.8×), val head mean 0.978, weak heads 10 → 3; determinism byte-equal. Wave 2 (2026-10-01) added char 4-grams + a 99-pair breadth corpus wave (1850 → 1949 pairs) with a controlled A/B (4-grams: +0.6 % exact, −4.7 % abstain vs 3-grams-only on the identical split) — **RELEASE GATE PASSED — `gatePassed: true` in the manifest, the first time in the model's history** (wave 6): compound/clarify/presetUnknown became recognition-and-handoff kinds (bare `{kind}` decode; the yardstick strips their open-vocabulary content the way engine fields are stripped — the resolver refuses the empty route and the deterministic layer owns parts/questions/suggestions). Final: train 99.7 %/0/8.2, **val 96.5 %/0/19.0, golden 100 %/0/14.9**, determinism byte-equal. A concurrent 15-sparse-head wave (compound part heads + clip/preset heads) was measured to collapse the shared trunk (val head mean 0.9893 → 0.9303) and was reverted to the 34-head release candidate — preserved in fbaf219b + a backup, with their tests behind `it.skipIf(!hasPartHeads)` for the day the corpus grows into them. **Wave 7 (corpus density 1949 → 2085, +100 sparse-family pairs) REVIVED the sparse heads**: the full 47-head trainer retrained clean on the enriched corpus — val head mean 0.9943 with zero weak heads, compound 44/44 train and 4/4 val exact, clips 30/30 + 3/3 golden — and the RELEASE GATE PASSED again on the 47-head artifact (val 95.5 % / 0 / 17.0 %, golden 100 % / 0 / 16.2 %, `gatePassed: true`); the part-head tests are un-skipped and active. **Wave 8 — ACTIVATION**: the loader default flipped to ON (explicit `pf:intent-model=off` opt-out persists; the manifest's gatePassed pin remains the real gate; the deterministic parsers stay the DEFAULT) — confirmed by `tests/e2e/14-intent-model-chip.spec.ts` (chromium 2/2: default boot reaches chip `ready` in ~8 s, OFF stays dark), and the SFT eval gained the clips toBar/ref/atBar convention normalization (LLM eval first-60: **96.7 % / wrongKind 0 / abstain 0**) **Rare-kind tail calibration (wave 3)**: diagnostics showed every wrongKind row is an out-of-scope-kind input guessed confidently with the abstain logit just below top-1 — a new `INTENT_MODEL_ABSTAIN_MARGIN = 2.0` decode tripwire cuts wrongKind 6 → 1 while RAISING attempted-exact to 69.1 % (golden 94.1 % / wrongKind 0; the abstained rows were mostly wrong attempts). An oversampling experiment that collapsed the direction head was rejected and reverted. Full verdict documented in `docs/INTENT-MINING-2026-09-30.md` (REVISED: the 35–40 % ceiling was a word-BoW ceiling, not a model-family ceiling), the val gate stays honestly FAILED, and the head remains inert by design — desktop keeps the SFT model (92.3 %), browser the deterministic layer. Margin-abstention was exonerated as a bottleneck (scan 1.0 → 0.1 moved exact by +4); `decodeIntentHeads` gained an optional margin override for mining tools with the production pin unchanged.

- **SUNO MODE button** — "one sentence to a finished track" UI mode plus dynamic song-form expansion (intro / build / drop / outro). Embedding shadow A/B harness for safe rollout. _(feat `e7d3071`, `add0f8b`)_
- **Audio reference intent** — `feat: make it sound like this WAV`. _(feat `daa7d8a`)_
- **Reference mix conditioning — the loudness half of "znej ako ref"** — the MATCH EQ matched tonal SHAPE only (level was "the loudness pass's job", but that pass was per-genre, never per-reference); `referenceLoudnessTrim` derives the level half from the retained reference's own BS.1770 integrated loudness (trim = ref LUFS − streaming target, clamped ±6 like the per-genre trim), with a plausibility window (−30…−5 LUFS — a −66 LUFS "reference" would ask for a −52 dB trim, so the level match declines). One MATCH REF click now installs tonal curve + loudness trim in ONE undo (`applyMasterMatchEqCommand` gained the optional trim); subsequent song generation recomputes the per-genre trim as before. Locked by `tests/reference-mix-conditioning.test.ts` (derivation, clamps, plausibility decline, one-undo round-trip).

- **Embedding-conditioned prior v2** — Phases D–F of the conditioning chain. _(feat `c7df20b`)_

- **Crate-digger wave** — jersey/Baltimore producer depth (dj lilman / kayy drizz / so dellirious / dj problem / dj delish / dj tim dolla; baltimore club parent genre at 125–135 with scottie b / k-swift / debonair samir / kw griff / rod lee / miss tonya / blaqstarr), UKG producers (salute / barry can't swim / bassline-niche school: dj q / t2 / burgaboy / jamie duggan / trc), hyperpop deconstruction (umru / felicita / easyfun / life sim / hdmird / shygirl / jockstrap / black dresses), hardcore revival (machine girl / alice gas / sewerslvt / goreshit → dnb.amen) and the sigilkore/witch-house underworld (luci4 / sellasouls / nosgov / sematary / ghost mountain / salem / crim3s / crystal castles / ic3peak).

- **First-class genre promotion: hyperpop + ukg** — the two lanes that outgrew their hosts are now genres: `hyperpop` (new `src/ai/grooves/hyperpop.ts`: Hyper/Glitch/Rage, 140–170, drop-first song form, trap-kick + chip-rim kit, dark tone, dry mix, 3 progressions) and `ukg` (new `src/ai/grooves/ukg.ts`: UK Garage/Bassline/Deep, 128–140 swung 2-step, groove-form song, deep-kick + soft-hat kit, pump at dance energy, 3 progressions). Full integration: `GENRES`/`Genre` union, `SONG_FORMS` + `GENRE_DEFAULT_BPM` (155 / 134), parser routing (`hyperpop`/`deconstructed club` → hyperpop; `ukg`/`uk garage`/`speed garage`/`bassline`/`2-step garage` → ukg, with guards keeping bare "hyper" a style and "two step dnb" dnb), `GENRE_KIT_SWAPS` + `GENRE_FEEL`, mix character (`dryGenre`, `pumpWanted`), harmony progressions, descriptions, 21 artist presets moved from trap/house onto their real genres. New `tests/genre-promotion.test.ts` locks the completeness contract.
